# China regional/urban variation journal expansion

### Research-purpose matching and poverty-threshold example — 2026-10-04

Audit `task-c843b1804831` repairs two reviewed usability gaps without
changing the architecture. Matching now accepts explicit research intent
and per-variation profile IDs, preserves missing fields for the intended
application, and labels unspecified uses as data-fit exploration. Joint
compatibility orders the recalled candidates before the display limit;
it does not replace institutional judgment with a causal-quality score.

The8-7 record now consistently distinguishes400-yuan eligibility from
actual designation and corrects retention to the below700 side. The
author-hosted [Lü2015 full text](https://www.xiaobolu.com/_files/ugd/265480_8331f98fb95544a58dbe2701f917f997.pdf)
was re-inspected at printedpp201–205. The generic five-before/five-after
and transfer-amount requirements were replaced with a designation-based
fuzzy-RD contract retaining historical status and continuity diagnostics.
Meng2013's abstract and publisher previews do not establish available
replication files or independently recover the2000 income endpoint;
the record now states that boundary instead of implying data access.
The prior official-source inspections remain attributed to their original
evidence; this audit does not claim a new official-roster verification.
No knowledge was deleted, no source PDF stored, and no count increase claimed.

### Broadband China: outcome-specific data fit — 2026-10-04

Audit `task-5ff1722ba693` re-inspected Cai and Wu's author-hosted
published paper, p14 Section6.1/footnotes11–13 and pp17–18 Tables7–9.
The existing record had required patents, registrations, subscribers
and GDP together for all uses, although the paper estimates separate
outcome regressions. Its default now describes the conditional innovation
application, and four compact profiles distinguish patent growth,
small-firm entry growth, GDP-per-capita growth and penetration growth.
Each retains the official-cohort/entity-crosswalk bridge; no unrelated
outcome dataset is made mandatory for the other applications.

The audit also corrects entry to **growth** in new registrations per100
residents below RMB2million in capital and dates the source-described
CNIPA/AiQiCha collections to December2021. Exact growth/zero handling,
baseline control years and historical registry reconstruction were not
verified. Section6.1 and conditional table notes describe different
controls, so the record preserves that difference and identifies which
table each profile follows rather than inventing a unified specification.
The117 official list entities are not independently crosswalked to the
paper's reported117 treated cities. No new variation, maturity promotion
or ready-count increment is claimed; no source PDF or microdata were stored.

### HSR recentered exposure: inspected replication bridge — 2026-10-04

Audit `task-22b826eb90ff` downloaded
[Zenodo8286785](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.8286785)'s public ZIP into
memory, inspected README_ECMA.pdf pp1–9, core network and outcome scripts,
the scenario processor and Table1, and checked selected input/map CSV
headers. No source programs were executed and no archive data were copied
into this repository. The existing HSR canonical now supplies the specific
ID and exposure-field bridge instead of describing the archive as uninspected.

This closes substantive gaps, not just access metadata. `cityid` derives
from GIS OBJECTID rather than an official prefecture identifier. The
recentered variable uses the mean of counterfactual **log** access, with
opening years permuted within link-count groups and the2003 pilot fixed.
The supplied Lines row codes 秦沈客运专线 at October12,2003; that author
coding corrects the previous no-pre2008-line implication but is not
independent official opening confirmation. The outcome is whole-prefecture
average employed staff and workers. Sustained jumps exceeding log(2)
cause missing growth, not simple winsorization; package comments distinguish
283 endpoint observations from275 surviving this filter, without execution
of those counts here.

The package contains raw inputs and scripts but no prepared scenario
outputs or final analysis_data.dta. A map-exposure CSV is not that final
sample. Its README describes a long local serial calculation and separate
cluster processing; these are author estimates, not tested timings. The
record remains grounded and conditional: external historical-code joins,
official line dates, numerical replication and outcome-specific validity
are not certified. No new variation or ready-count increment is claimed.

The audit removes one false access blocker and adds the concrete historical

### Spousal state-job candidate: bundled reform boundary — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-8738606bf794` inspected the PKU precursor's Online AppendixI.2,
PDF57–59, appendix printed3–5. It explicitly combines the Company Law,
foreign-exchange/trade restructuring and tax restructuring as the post1993
institutionalization episode. The coastal classification reflects economic
responsiveness, not a legal list of places uniquely assigned one reform.
The paper-used DDD is recoverable, but cannot identify any one component's
effect separately. This is a substantive canonical-boundary gap: the current
repository represents an instrument under a specific implementation and
assignment, not a broad transition-era policy family. Do not manufacture a
Company-Law treatment by relabelling the paper's multi-reform comparison.

An independent official chronology was actually inspected visually in the
[CSRC1995No2 gazette](https://www.csrc.gov.cn/csrc/c100024/c1492397/1492397/files/543a9663ebb241219ef78e80b5a25355.pdf),
PDF4, printed1: the March29,1995 notice `证委发〔1995〕5号` states that the
Company Law became effective July1,1994. This contradicts the precursor's
AppendixI.2 statement that all three measures became effective January1,1994.
It does not demonstrate erroneous regression coding: the observed pre/post
waves are1993/1997, not monthly1994. The gazette opening establishes the law's
date only, not the full legal provisions, the paper's spouse exposure or a
coastal assignment. NPC/court text endpoints and Zhejiang gazette were
inaccessible; no primary law body was read from those endpoints.

The survey provider's old homepage redirects to [CHNS](https://chns.cpc.unc.edu).
Its [ID Variables](https://chns.cpc.unc.edu/data/documentation/idvar/)
documentation was read in full: new identifiers replace old IDs throughout
the files; `IDind` is stable across waves, `HHID` identifies households, and
`COMMID` joins community-year information. Use `IDind × WAVE` for individual
longitudinal merges and household rosters for actual partner links; household
membership alone does not identify a spouse. The
[Special LINE Numbers](https://chns.cpc.unc.edu/data/documentation/lineno/)
page identifies newly added members, so neither a constant row order nor a
new-member code alone should stand for unchanged marriage. The
[FAQ](https://chns.cpc.unc.edu/data/faq/) explicitly withholds county/city
names and locations and offers reviewed, paid administrative-code linkages.
This does not block the paper's province-level comparison; it conditions
local-policy or county-risk extensions. The current
[study-population page](https://chns.cpc.unc.edu/about/design/study/)
lists later expanded coverage, not the seven-province baseline's weights or
national representativeness. No data files, questionnaires or final journal
replication code were inspected; do not certify exact reproduction.

Disposition: blocked at institutional-identity admission, retaining the useful
paper-specific exposure and join guidance. Reopen with an application or
institutional evidence that connects this comparison to a bounded instrument
and implementation regime; merely obtaining another abstract or a convenient
January1994 date is insufficient. Nonrandom spouses, household-only clustering
for seven provinces, differential entrepreneurial trends and attrition remain
research conditions, not proof that the original study is invalid. No canonical
record added, no existing knowledge modified or removed.

### Market transition and spousal state-job exposure — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-b1288ebf42f5` retains a source for resolution, not canonical
publication. Li, Lin, Yi and Zhang's *Dual-track in the family: How spousal
state employment fuels entrepreneurship*,
[DOI10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103899](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103899),
is listed by [coauthor Yi](https://sites.google.com/view/junjianyi/research)
as JDE184(2027),103899; the
[Tongji September2026 notice](https://sem.tongji.edu.cn/semen/28045.html)
confirms publication and the entrepreneurial question. Neither source
establishes final treatment coding. Publisher body returned403 and SSRN5138687
delivery was inaccessible. The lawful
[PKU2025 discussion-paper page](https://nsd.pku.edu.cn/xzyj/cbw/tlg1/tlg2025/539846.htm)
links an [89-page PDF](https://nsd.pku.edu.cn/docs/20250215160846249219.pdf),
*State Employment, Entrepreneurship, and the Role of Family Insurance*,
E2025002, February15,2025. Cover and printed pp7–17 were inspected in full:
Sections2,3.1–3.3,4.1 and equations1–3. This is a precursor application,
not an inspected final journal method.

The manuscript actually uses `Coastal × Post × SpouseState`, not randomized
spousal employment or staggered assignment of a1993 program. `SpouseState`
uses1989 state-sector employment, imputing missing baseline sector for active
workers from the earliest available pre-reform wave (footnote5). The baseline
uses CHNS1989,1991,1993 and1997;2000/2004 are extensions. Footnote11 identifies
coastal Jiangsu, Shandong, Guangxi versus inland Guizhou, Henan, Hubei, Hunan;
Liaoning/Heilongjiang are excluded for wave gaps. Both spouses must be18–60
and neither a student nor retiree. The reported sample has2,132 households,
4,264 individuals and14,864 individual-year observations. Entrepreneurship
means primary non-farming self-employment, with or without employees, not
necessarily a newly registered innovative firm. Equation2 includes individual
and province-survey-year fixed effects, baseline controls interacted with post
and coast, and household-clustered errors.1993 is the event-study reference.
Coastal and spousal status are exposure proxies under identifying assumptions,
not statutory eligibility. Inland households also face national reforms.

Resolve the exact institutional bundle and primary dates, spouse/person
longitudinal joins and employer-sector definitions, inference with seven
provinces, partner selection and group-specific trends, and any final-version
change. The manuscript's claim that several laws all took effect January1,1994
must be checked instrument by instrument; it cannot become a verified common
effective date. Its description of CHNS as nationally representative also
requires checking survey-provider coverage, rather than generalizing a
seven-province analysis to all mainland households. Preserve this as one
candidate exposure, not separate shocks for entrepreneurship, innovation or
family insurance. No canonical file or data-repository asset was written.

### Manufacturing dynamism: measurement is not policy assignment — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-e15e1f1bd90d` inspected Brandt, Van Biesebroeck, Wang and
Zhang's published *Where has all the dynamism gone? Productivity growth in
China's manufacturing sector, 1998–2013*, JDE181(2026),103720,
[DOI10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103720](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103720).
The [author-hosted published PDF](https://www.yifanzhang.net/_files/ugd/be9597_6316a800fb4a4c54b16202b54feca474.pdf)
has23pages; the cover records online publication January20,2026.
Inspected Sections1–3, Section5.2 and Section6, printed pp1–5,13–14,17.
Its actual application corrects survey composition and measures productivity,
not exposure to a policy. Section3 estimates source-to-target density weights;
those weights are sampling corrections, not treatment probabilities or an
instrument. Section5.2 explicitly distinguishes survey rotation from true
entry/exit and uses firm age for young-firm contributions. Section6 leaves
separating demand, stimulus and regulatory explanations to future research.
Mentioning VAT reforms or the2008 stimulus therefore does not establish that
this paper used their assignment for identification. Outcome trends, data
discrepancies and the2007 period split do not supply a new shock.

Disposition: skipped for canonical variation collection, not a judgment that
the paper lacks research value. Data-quality and reconstruction knowledge is
potentially relevant to the separate data repository, but no reciprocal record
or dataset was written there. No candidate or canonical file added. County-border
land-allocation DOI10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103920, encountered in the same bounded
recall pass, was reconciled to existing
`china-county-industry-industrial-land-discount-border` and not re-screened.

Cheng and Gawande, JLE67(2),265-294(2024), DOI10.1086/727444.

interacted with an annual post indicator beginning2006. Footnote6 defines the

stronger predicted reform impact. This is a continuous regional exposure, not

silently replace2006 with2007 or describe the law as a constitutional amendment

The draft aggregates registry entry cohorts to prefecture-years1998-2012,
excludes subsidiaries and incomplete location/type/industry/capital observations,

production; paid-in capital is not total assets. Employment has substantial

population includes agricultural industries, but the retained research question
is enterprise development, not a new agriculture campaign or financial outcome.

was inspected: OA1-5 cover outliers, balance, confounding policies, alternate
baseline years and neighboring exposure; OA7 uses two-digit industries.
The linked10532Data.zip request returned403; no replication files were read.

definition, survival censoring and overlap with property-law candidates
`candidate-a88a045522ed`, `candidate-88e7b08906ac`, and the existing movable
collateral record. Multiple outcomes do not justify another canonical mechanism.
Keep selection into initial ownership composition and differential trends as
identification risks, not resolved by the manuscript's confidence in its controls.

destinations conditional on rotation, not random assignment; its prose says

AppendixC.1's prose says province-year fixed effects while TableA.3's note
says province-pair. Preserve the discrepancies; do not silently harmonize them.
Main Tables1-2 use pair clustering, lagged links and five-year differences;
Section3.3 distinguishes link formation from dissolution.

Screen `task-6231147279ce` inspected Jiang and Mei, *Mandarins make
markets*, JDE147(2020), DOI10.1016/j.jdeveco.2020.102524, in the
[author-hosted published text](https://www.junyanjiang.com/uploads/5/8/1/9/58193547/jiangmei_mmm.pdf),
Sections2.1-2.2 and3.1, printedpp3-5. No canonical admission.

The directed province-year exposure indicates whether A's current party
secretary previously served as secretary or governor in B. It is not an
arrival-only pulse or city-level investment exposure. The application studies
A-to-B railway cargo, with pair, origin-year and destination-year fixed
effects and a first-differenced alternative. Railway freight is a trade proxy,
not all domestic sales. Biographies come from CPED; cargo comes from the
China Railway Yearbook; Tibet is excluded from cargo pairs.

The paper argues destinations are quasi-random; this remains a research
assumption, not an institutional fact. Resolve annual office coding, prior-post
selection, institutional authority, appendix diagnostics and lawful data joins
before publication. Destination selection and changing bilateral trade costs
remain principal threats. This regional-integration candidate is distinct from
the city-level Moving Umbrella candidate, but requires mechanism-level audit.

above five million registered residents are excluded as origins. Pair,
origin-time and port-time effects address specified confounding; network
placement remains assumption-dependent.

### Bankruptcy-court resolution: inauguration is not universal case routing — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-c100ed73d292` follows candidate-b5697df75a46, CER2026
DOI10.1016/j.chieco.2026.102706. New primary evidence makes the institutional
boundary clearer but does not replace the unavailable research application.
The [Shanghai High Court inauguration account](https://www.hshfy.sh.cn/shfy/gweb2017/channel_tp_view.jsp?pa=aaWQ9MjA0ODQPdcssz&zd=xwzx),
February1,2019, was inspected in full. The named bankruptcy court was
established inside the Third Intermediate People's Court, following approval
to adjust its internal institutions. It is not a new standalone people's court.
The account's broad statement of concentrated jurisdiction must be read with
the later operative rules, not used to classify every local case identically.

The [Shanghai bankruptcy jurisdiction implementation rules](https://www.hshfy.sh.cn/shfy/web/xxnr.jsp?pa=aaWQ9MjAxMjg2NTUmeGg9MSZsbWRtPWxtOTEwz)
were inspected in full, including the June4,2019 signature, May24 adoption
and Articles1-4. Their authority is the SPC approval Fa[2019]4;
Article4 makes the rules effective upon issuance. Article1 covers registered
Shanghai enterprises' party-initiated proceedings except those assigned to
the financial and railway transport courts, specified enforcement-to-bankruptcy
transfers, derivative litigation, cross-border matters and appeals. Article2
assigns enterprises registered in Minhang, Xuhui, Huangpu and Yangpu to the
railway transport court and also covers local SOEs, subject to its stated
exceptions. Article3 retains district-court handling of specified local
enforcement transfers and already-accepted compulsory-liquidation matters
that subsequently enter bankruptcy. These provisions describe differentiated
jurisdiction, not universal exposure to one adjudicating bench.

Consequently, institution availability, ex-ante creditor expectations and
actual case adjudication are different exposure concepts. A listed firm's
head-office city alone does not reconstruct registered-enterprise category,
court jurisdiction and procedural route. An annual city-availability design
can still be meaningful, but its meaning must come from the paper's encoding;
it must not be rewritten as the effect of universally receiving new-court
adjudication. February inauguration and June rules also cannot be collapsed
into a verified daily treatment date. This is an analytical encoding implication,
not evidence that the unpublished-to-us CER methods are wrong.

The Guangdong white paper's PartII, sectionII, also documents a later2019
change toward intermediate-court jurisdiction, with designated lower-court
exceptions and restrictions on routing listed/financial/group-consolidation
cases to lower courts. Its November guidance is reported there, but a
[Guangzhou court-hosted copy](https://www.gzcourt.gov.cn/ck321/ck326/2020/09/25160101333.html)
could not be read (HTTP420). Do not treat a secondary date summary as
inspection of that original instruction. Final-body/appendix searches under
the DOI and authors did not recover an open version; no messages were sent.

Finish blocked, preserving the new primary jurisdiction evidence. Reopen when
the CER full methods, court/date inventory and firm exposure joins can be
inspected. This task adds neither a canonical case nor a ready count; it closes
a real institutional ambiguity while leaving exact research assignment,
comparison cohorts and investment-outcome construction unresolved.

or candidate for this experimental assignment. The
[UCSB green publication inventory](https://faculty.econ.ucsb.edu/~tedb/GreenTables/JDevelopmentEconFolder/JDE_january2020.html)
links a public [78-page manuscript with appendix](https://www.dropbox.com/s/22v13s859pz75zb/migration_final.pdf?dl=0).
It was read in memory, not stored: Sections2-4, printedpp14-25, and AppendixD,
appendixpp6-7. This is an inspected manuscript, not an inspected publisher PDF.

The paper reports county-stratified village randomization: officials nominate
five poor villages in each of ten counties in Gansu, Sichuan, Henan, Hunan and
Shandong; researchers select three for treatment and two for control. The
August2010 baseline precedes introduction from September2010, with most lending
starting January-June2011. One Hunan county begins lending only June2012 and
is excluded from the main analysis. The July2012 follow-up retrospectively
measures2011 outcomes, whereas baseline outcomes concern2009. The original
1500 households become1351 re-interviewed; the main sample is1234 households
in45 villages/nine counties. Preserve both attrition and the implementation
exclusion instead of describing the analysed sample as the original allocation.

Treatment is the village offer, not borrowing: paper-reported take-up is28%.
Committees choose lending terms; loans support income-generating activities,
typically with group liability, no collateral and one annual repayment. Equation1
is a household ITT ANCOVA with baseline outcomes/covariates and county-stratum
effects, village-clustered errors. The author also describes within-stratum
randomization inference. Migration is noncommuting work away from the specified
home administrative area for at least ten days by members aged16-65. Asset
heterogeneity is not itself randomized, nor is the exclusion of a late county.

AppendixD exposes a useful data limit: transport prices are2016 prices deflated
to2010, not observed2010/2012 tickets; across-province destinations are proxied
by the two most common destination provinces. Rent proxies rely on capital-city
rents and assumed20-square-metre per-person housing. These are mechanism-cost
proxies, not the randomized treatment. Rainfall in AppendixE is an auxiliary
instrument for assets, not a second canonical case being admitted by this screen.

Retain a china-variation candidate for development finance and rural-to-urban
resource allocation, not an agriculture-output treatment. Resolve original
experimental allocation/protocol and implementation evidence, exact village and
household identifiers, companion-paper overlap, attrition/full-sample diagnostics,
and access to the survey/replication materials before canonical admission. Do not
convert the nationwide2006 rollout into the experiment or lending take-up into
random assignment. No canonical changed; ready116.

### Upwind coal-power migration IV: author body recovered, version gap narrowed — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-064f87ee2f79` revisited blocked candidate-03570eaab8f6 using the
newly inspected [author's June2023,94-page manuscript](https://econgaurav.github.io/papers/Productivity_Consequences.pdf).
Sections2-4, printedpp6-16, equation2/Figure1, AppendixA TablesA5-A11
and Data AppendixF ppA39-A42 were inspected in memory. This recovers the
previously missing body, not the final2025 body. The [AEA publication page](https://www.aeaweb.org/articles?id=10.1257/app.20220655)
identifies AEJ:Applied17(2),184-224,2025; its full-text link still returns an
access page. The [official51-page appendix](https://www.aeaweb.org/articles/materials/22709)
was independently inspected through its text, especially A1-A9 andF.

Working-version equation2 constructs city exposure as the sum across qualifying
plants of `coal_consumption / ((angle + 1) * distance)`. It describes annual
prevailing wind and annual plant coal consumption, plants with installed
capacity strictly above1million kW, and a baseline100-500km band around the
city. Alternative200-600km and400-800km bands belong to robustness, not the
baseline. This is a continuous city exposure used to instrument logPM2.5,
not a binary policy treatment or assigned access to electricity. Coal consumption
is not interchangeable with generation or installed capacity. Exact angle units,
bearing convention, plant/city coordinate vintage, and baseline input year/window
remain to be recovered from final methods or construction documentation; do not
infer them from the map or the phrase "annual". The appendix's upwind-sector
counting rule is not a replacement for the continuous weighting formula.

The working-version Table1 uses761,548 individuals across332 cities in2015,
with exposure attached to hukou-origin city. Migration means residing away from
that city for more than six months, and skill compares some-college-or-above
with high-school-or-below. Current residence and hukou origin are separate
joins. CLDS retrospective2008-2016 city changes are an alternative outcome,
not the same migration definition or observation window. The paper labels the
2015 source a census; preserve that attribution rather than assert it is a full
decennial census. City-boundary crosswalks and restricted microdata access remain
necessary. No individual data were obtained or stored.

There is a substantive version difference. Official appendix TablesA1-A4
explicitly add inverse-distance-weighted average coal consumption within500km
to specified controls; the corresponding working-version descriptions do not.
Final table numbering also differs. Preserve final locators rather than copy
working-version table references into a2025 application. Final AppendixF still
attributes plant inputs to the Electric Power/Energy yearbooks and manual
plant establishment/angle/distance collection, without settling the missing
construction conventions. Natural wind does not make endogenous plant siting,
coal use, weather amenities or regional electricity/economic linkages disappear.
Old-plant, distance-band, baseline-economy and placebo checks are reported tests,
not proof of exclusion. Population re-sorting also has general-equilibrium
spillovers; destination net populations are not untreated city outcomes.

The [official V2 replication deposit](https://doi.org/10.3886/E192181V2) now
exposes a readable file inventory, including README.pdf, Program/Code_DataClean/
Do_file_2015mig.do and Program/Code_Table/Table1.do. File-list inspection is
not code inspection: the README download redirects to ICPSR login, and no
README or code contents were recovered. No access controls were bypassed.
Disposition remains blocked at admission. The next useful step is authorized
inspection of those files and the actual IV inputs/construction, or an accessible
final/accepted body that closes the angle, time and version gaps. The formula

### Registered-capital trading rights: body recovered, admission gap — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-3147fd574bba` recovered the final16-page REStat body through
[Tsinghua's2025 publication inventory](https://eng.pbcsf.tsinghua.edu.cn/info/1119/3038.htm),
which links the [institution-hosted typeset paper](https://www.pbcsf.tsinghua.edu.cn/__local/F/CA/E9/BFA1FE7EFA23409795988C47234_2327CA2F_16C3FA.pdf).
Printedpp1277-1288, SectionsII-V, Table1 and equations1-3 were inspected;
this supersedes the screen's body-access blocker, not its archived provenance.
The supplemental appendix remains uninspected. No copyrighted PDF was stored.

The original [2001 qualification notice](https://www.mofcom.gov.cn/zcfb/dwmygl/art/2001/art_07b53ba3f7c84549af86a6f6b6d31423.html),
外经贸贸发[2001]370号, dated/effective July10, was read in full. SectionsI-II,
III(II), IV and X distinguish manufacturing self-trade rights from general
foreign-trade circulation rights. Self-trade eligibility requires capital of
RMB3million, or2million for central/western/minority areas, or1million for
research institutes, high-tech and mechanical/electrical producers, plus
registration, taxation and disqualification conditions. FIEs, specified other
enterprise categories, SEZs and Pudong follow separate rules. Capital eligibility
does not itself confer a certificate or remove product restrictions.

The official [2003 notice reproduction](https://www.chinatax.gov.cn/chinatax/n810341/n810765/n812198/200309/c1205621/content.html),
商贸发[2003]254号 in 国税函[2003]1019号, was inspected at SectionsI-VI.
It is dated July30 and takes effect September1,2003. Manufacturing self-trade
capital falls to RMB0.5million; circulation rights retain a different threshold.
SectionI enumerates the central/western jurisdictions; SectionVI removes
specified special-zone geographic restrictions. This is historical law,
currently marked repealed, not a current eligibility guide.

Body-only research recovery: the2002 eastern baseline excludes the other
threshold regimes. Paid-in ASIF capital proxies registered capital; SectionII
gives inconsistent pre2006/pre2014 legal-equality wording, so do not promote
that proxy equivalence to verified law. Product production and ASIF share firm
IDs; the customs linkage is described as manually combined but not reproduced.
Trade is positive customs exports or imports, not just exports. TFPQ uses
physical production and industry production functions, not revenue TFP.
The adjustment window and density polynomial are selected by fivefold
cross-validation with excess mass equalling missing mass; rounding at50/100
multiples is modeled. Outside-window outcome regressions allow a cutoff jump,
sector effects and rounding. Density-weighted outcome differences recover
capital-adjusting firms' treated/counterfactual means. Capital movers are not
observed random assignment; no-adjustment smoothness and no downward response
are identifying assumptions. The trade-effect ratio additionally needs exclusion:
capital adjustment must not independently change the proposed outcome.

SectionVC reports balance-sheet and unchanged-capital checks, but supplier and
customer channels are not measured. SectionVD cautions that2003/2004 reforms
contaminate a permanent-treatment reading of later outcomes. These checks are
reported body descriptions; the referenced appendix results were not inspected.

Disposition: blocked at admission, retaining candidate-fddda24415dd. Recover
AppendicesA-B,D-E or equivalent author replication material: exact eastern and
M&E/HTE/SEZ/Pudong exclusions, customs-to-ASIF join and unmatched-firm handling,
production-function construction and below-threshold trade explanations. Legal
exceptions make these substantive assignment/data gaps, not formatting debt.
Do not create separate outcome records or publish an extracted draft. Ready114.

Screen `task-1ae6ae9f8ef7` extends the existing REStat firm/trade lane, not
the journal boundary. Li, Lu and Wang, *Productivity Gains from Trade: Bunching
Estimates from Trading Rights in China*, REStat107(2025),1275-1290,

assignment in the current records or candidate ledger.

The inspected [authors' explanation](https://www.voxdev.org/topic/trade/learning-trading-how-reforming-trade-policy-boosted-firm-productivity-china),
sections “Assessing China's trade regulatory policy” and “What changes when
firms gain trading rights,” reports a RMB3million registered-capital cutoff
for most East China domestic firms in2002. Firms adjust capital to qualify;
the study uses bunching and counterfactual outcome distributions, not an

### Gasoline-IV rollout: primary assignment recovered, date panel still open — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-028b9b589305` inspected
[NDRC 发改价格〔2013〕1845号](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/tz/201309/t20130923_963944.html),
signed September16, posted September23,2013. ItemII permits province- or
city-level implementation with provincial approval and puts the gasoline-IV
transition deadline at the end of2013. This is not a common September2013
treatment date. ItemI specifies the national gasoline-III-to-IV cost adjustment
of RMB290/ton; itemIII separately handles earlier pilot pricing. ItemsIV–V
require supply coordination and production/wholesale/retail inspections,
not proof that every outlet complied. The official
[NDRC price-policy Q&A](https://www.nea.gov.cn/2013-09/24/c_132745930.htm)
distinguishes gasoline III150ppm, IV50ppm and V10ppm sulfur limits.

The full body of
[江苏苏政办发〔2012〕199号](https://www.jiangsu.gov.cn/art/2012/12/7/art_46737_2669563.html)
was inspected before later requests became inaccessible. Signed December7,2012,
sectionsI–II set December30 gasoline-IV supply for Wuxi, Changzhou, Suzhou,
Nantong, Yangzhou, Zhenjiang and Taizhou; Nanjing had already upgraded.
Replacement proceeds through December29 at unchanged prices. Wholesalers
submit plans by December20, outlets label the new fuel and authorities inspect
from December30. SectionIV highlights border-area compliance. The concurrent
county-urban diesel expansion is stageIII, not dieselIV. Do not assign this
date to all Jiangsu prefectures or treat replacement as a perfectly sharp jump.

The published paper's sections3.1–3.2 were read, not only its abstract:
daily prefectural enforcement is joined to station pollution; later adopters
form the DD comparison. Its stacked observations pool pollutants, not adoption
cohorts. RDiT additionally assumes continuity at the date and models serial
correlation. These are paper-reported design choices, not certificates of
random timing or complete compliance.

**Admission decision: blocked, not extracted.** The full prefecture-date panel
has not been recovered, and the Guangdong local-standard crosswalk used by
the paper has not been verified against its operative notices. National legal
delegation plus one local example does not close the application-wide treatment
construction. A follow-up should recover provincial effective circulars and
the Guangdong local-code equivalence, retaining announcement, replacement,
mandatory-sale and paper-coded dates separately. Jiangsu's verified example
cannot be substituted for the paper-wide exposure or counted as a second
variation simply to increase ready totals. No copyrighted PDF or data was saved.

### Exporter tariffs: inspected code establishes a pooled instrument — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-004b02d929f7` read the public version2 preparation script in
full and the table script's main specifications and appendix headings/event
construction. Files were fetched through the deposit's public interface;
nothing was executed or stored locally. Stable source links:
[preparation](https://data.mendeley.com/public-files/datasets/nsbjkhrc7t/files/4a0bab55-defd-4ea8-aec5-99ef348065ed/file_downloaded),
[tables](https://data.mendeley.com/public-files/datasets/nsbjkhrc7t/files/deadb32d-f4c4-48c6-b695-df094a970cc5/file_downloaded),
[treated products](https://data.mendeley.com/public-files/datasets/nsbjkhrc7t/files/fd0a5fb9-f69a-45ae-81d6-320a3eabea9c/file_downloaded),
[matched products](https://data.mendeley.com/public-files/datasets/nsbjkhrc7t/files/130380db-2df8-44c7-94f2-b78d17f38369/file_downloaded).

Preparation lines16–24 aggregate transaction value and first-unit quantity
to Chinese HS8-by-year-month, derive unit value and logs, and drop missing
log prices. Lines26–35 add Chinese retaliation, month-level onset and a
January–March2019 suspension adjustment. Lines38–65 merge HS8 targeted
products and HS6/4 steel/aluminum inputs, with explicit overrides for two
aluminum codes. Lines67–81 drop flagged exclusions and unmatched products,
then define treatment for `alsteel`, `34b`, `16b` and `200b`, with post from
the effective month. Thus the baseline binary exposure pools Section232
and Section301, not Section301 alone. The US panel first restricts customs
destination code502; the world panel aggregates across destinations. This
is a product-month application, not a firm-level assignment inferred from
the title.

Table2 lines27–36 use the three logged outcomes, treatment-by-post and
Chinese retaliatory rates, restricted to nonmissing statutory tariffs;
fixed effects include product, year-month, HS6 seasonal/festival controls
and HS6-specific trends, with HS8 clustering. Table3 uses the world panel.
AppendixA6 constructs pooled relative-month indicators. These inspected
specifications establish actual use, not the credibility of parallel trends
or the interpretation of a pooled coefficient as a single instrument.

The small public treatment input was read in memory:4621 distinct Chinese
HS8 codes, split810/192/3619 across34b/16b/200b;138 exclusion flags. These
are author-mapped Chinese product counts, not counts of statutory US lines.
It stores July6, August24 and September24,2018 onset and respective initial
rates25%,25%,10%. The matched input contains7043 unique Chinese HS8 codes
but no US-code column: it is a matched-product whitelist, not the complete

### Typhoon follow-up: per-storm maximum is explicit only in risk simulation — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-28c77ae1d98b` revisits `candidate-63c2306abc7c`. The
[Birmingham DP15-11](https://repec.cal.bham.ac.uk/pdf/15-11.pdf) was
retrieved and inspected in memory at printedpp7,11-17,28-30; no PDF was
stored. These passages narrow, but do not close, the annual-encoder gap.

SectionV, printedp29, explicitly takes each cell's maximum damage index
within each **synthetic storm**. Printedp28 constructs hypothetical years
by drawing storms from climatology-specific Poisson frequencies. This is
the risk simulation, not an explicit instruction to maximize or sum
historical storms into the main cell-year regressor. SectionIII's printed
pp16-17 instead uses annual cell damage with cell/year effects and
county-city clustering, without that bridge in the inspected text.
SectionII.A, printedp7, defines60 coastal administrative units among340
by administrative centroid within50km of shore; it does not select only
cells individually within50km. Printedpp11-13 evaluate modelled winds at
cell centroids, with gust/friction assumptions and an estimated radius
bounded8-150km. Those geographies and thresholds must remain separate.

[Analytical inference] The remaining issue is not whether wind fields can
be computed. It is which historical storm/time aggregation generates the
paper-used annual contrast. A simulation's per-storm maximum cannot supply
that missing rule. The earlier calibration inconsistencies and historical
administrative-polygon vintage remain unresolved. No guessed annual
encoder, modern-city backfill or ex-post damage measure is substituted.
Keep the candidate blocked until the historical encoder is recovered from
an inspectable accepted/final text, appendix or construction documentation.

recommendation followed by MOST determination and approval, with subsequent
local implementation plans. Provincial pilots are also supported, rather
than automatically made national pilots. SectionIV(3) provides priority for
qualifying research, infrastructure and talent projects through existing
funding channels, not a uniform grant at designation. SectionIV(4) requires
annual self-assessment and contemplates wider monitoring participation.

The [monitoring attachment](https://www.most.gov.cn/xxgk/xinxifenlei/fdzdgknr/fgzc/gfxwj/gfxwj2010before/201004/W020100415601478284990.doc)
lists innovation inputs, enterprise innovation, commercialization, high-tech
industry, public-benefit technology and innovation environment. It includes
R&D inputs, patents, contracts, productivity and environmental outcomes.
It supplies no city-year approval roster, eligibility cutoff, numerical
selection weights or automatic qualification threshold. The paper's
patent-value innovation index is not thereby verified as this official
monitoring measure. Monitoring participation is not national-pilot admission.

**Decision.** This primary source establishes a selective administrative
assignment and implementation sequence; it does not reconcile the study's47
treated cities to MOST approvals, NDRC overall-plan approvals or district-level
pilots. Preserve approval exposure as a possible intention-to-treat object,
but do not impute prefecture-wide treatment from a named district or substitute
April2010 guidance for every designation date. The remaining bridge is the
paper-used roster, its spatial aggregation and agency-specific start years.
Candidate remains blocked, with no canonical admission. Future recovery should
target individual approval notices and the article's treatment table rather
than retry these now-readable attachments or rebuild the same broad policy
background. Ready is unchanged.

online date. No paper file is stored in the repository.

foreign ownership, or proof that every included stock received equal demand.

Section4.1 reports568 initially eligible stocks,519 with usable returns and
characteristics, and440 matched treatment stocks. The initial control pool
contains324 unconnected Shanghai and1318 Shenzhen A-shares. Logit propensity

without replacement, caliper0.20. The final440 controls include77 Shanghai
and363 Shenzhen stocks. Thus neither a Shanghai-only control definition nor
an all-firms annual DID represents this paper's main application.

Equation2 interacts eligibility with Shanghai beta. Event windows are trading
days(-1,+1),(-2,+2),(-3,+3). Table4 has880 observations and industry-clustered

Printedp8 specifies SIZE at October2014, market/liquidity variables over
November2013-October2014, and BM/ROA/LEV at2013 year-end. The matching
description's October2014 wording should not erase these variable vintages.
Equations3-4 instead compare post-announcement(0,+10) average turnover or
intraday volatility with the recent-month baseline; these are applications of
the same assignment, not additional variations.

Resolve should inspect the original April10 and November10 joint notices,
the launch-vintage eligibility/exclusion rules and dated568-stock list; then
connect exchange-qualified security codes and trading dates to CSMAR returns,
accounting data, benchmarks and, where needed,5-minute prices. Today's
eligible-stock page cannot silently replace a2014 roster. Keep Shenzhen2016
outside this candidate's identity until its separate rule and announcement
clock are grounded. Anticipation after April, index-membership selection,
exchange-specific news, overlapping exposures and common event-day dependence
remain substantive identification concerns. Matching and adjustment do not
prove a pure demand channel. Section5.5 addresses beta-window contamination;

### Coastal typhoon wind-field exposure and local activity — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-70da08e3fbf5` retains Elliott, Strobl and Sun's China
typhoon application for identity resolution. [Birmingham's journal metadata](https://research.birmingham.ac.uk/en/publications/the-local-impact-of-typhoons-on-economic-activity-in-china-a-view/)
was inspected: JUE88,50-66(2015),

was read in memory at printedpp7-23; printedp14 Equation7 was visually
checked. Web PDF access failed, but direct retrieval succeeded. This
is inspected working-version evidence, not an inspected published PDF.

Reported construction: RSMC six-hourly tracks interpolated to three hours;
110 storms reaching typhoon strength and passing within500km of China's
coast in1992-2010. A Holland/Boose wind-field model supplies wind at
nightlight-cell centroids. Damage proxy f=v^3/(1+v^3), with
v=max(V-93,0)/(278-93), uses km/hour, not observed property destruction.
The main regression compares annual cell exposure with cell/year effects
and city-clustered errors. Coastal administrative units have centroids
within50km of shore. The draft retains563,192 cells, excludes1992 and
keeps positive light values plus zeros immediately following a positive.

[Analytical inference] Resolve annual aggregation across storm times and
storms, mainland-only boundary joins and final-version construction before
admission. The draft's later Vhalf=93 statement conflicts with its278
baseline; do not reproduce it as an alternative calibration. Distinguish
modelled wind damage from rainfall/surge, and nightlight loss from income
or welfare loss. Time-invariant risk controls do not remove time-varying
adaptation; selection on light and common storm dependence remain relevant.
Future simulated storms are a risk exercise, not observed treatment.
No canonical edit or ready increment at screen.

[DOI10.1016/j.chieco.2024.102327](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.chieco.2024.102327).
The [institution-hosted published PDF](https://research-management.mq.edu.au/ws/portalfiles/portal/400194034/399583168.pdf)
was read through the web PDF text: printedpp4-6,8,13-14, Sections3-4,
Equations1-2, Table3, TablesA1-A5. Direct retrieval returned403; no local
copy or successful visual render was obtained.

Reported construction: annual predicted PM2.5 uses inversion temperatures,
wind speed/direction and inversion occurrences. Footnote15 names NASA
M2I6NPANA_5.12.4, six-hourly inputs and adjacent1000-to850hPa layers.
City PM2.5 uses V5.GL.02. Urban CHARLS2011/2013/2015/2018 observations
exclude agriculture, solo/unpaid-family work and migration tendencies.
The baseline has2016 observations, not2016 persons. Individual/year effects
and household-clustered errors accompany2SLS; Table3 reports F38.920.

[Analytical inference] Weather-predicted pollution is not a newly enacted
policy or a binary treated-city list. Natural weather does not establish
exclusion: wind and atmospheric conditions can affect economic activity
through other channels. Household clustering does not by itself address
shared city weather. Outcome differences between OLS and IV do not prove
causality; sequential mediation requires additional assumptions.

Resolve the prediction equation, annual aggregation, wind representation,
city/grid join and sample-selection cutoffs. Compare the existing Beijing
air-travel inversion record before deciding whether this joint annual
construction is distinct or an additional application. Frequency/outcome
alone cannot justify a new record. Retain in the candidate layer; no ready
increment or canonical edit at screen.

### Cleanup resolution: designation loss is not plant closure — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-5b062dcd8f87`, linked to `candidate-2461239f3f06`, closes
several institutional ambiguities but leaves the paper-specific assignment
unrecovered. The candidate remains blocked; no canonical admission follows.

The [original State Council notice](http://www.gov.cn/zhengce/content/2008-03/28/content_2471.htm)
was inspected in full, ArticlesI-VI, via direct HTTP; HTTPS web access returned
403. 国办发〔2003〕70号 is signed30July2003, not its2008 web-publication date.
ArticleIII requires rectification first for unauthorized or unfiled zones,
termination for those lacking construction conditions, projects or funding,
and provincial review/State Council approval for necessary retained zones.
It separately reviews lawful zones and unauthorized expansion. ArticleV
governs expansion, relocation and upgrades. Thus lack of a later approval
is not itself the statutory test for closure. ArticleVI's end2003 target and
March2004 acceptance-report target are deadlines, not proof of uniform actual
city implementation.

The [NDRC2005Announcement30](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/gg/200506/t20050617_961626.html)
was read in full, body and13June2005 signature; its zone-list attachment was
not inspected. Passing review restores normal construction-land supply;
unreviewed zones cannot add new construction land. This establishes an
operational land-supply channel, not a new universal plant-shutdown date or
the candidate's provincial approval membership.

The [Shandong cleanup summary on Weihai's official site](https://fgw.weihai.gov.cn/art/2009/9/10/art_4198_256085.html)
was inspected in full, SectionsI-IV. SectionII(1) explicitly reports that82
previously subprovincial parks received regularizing approval. SectionsII(2)
andIII describe selection by regional industry, existing development,
planning and applications. [Analytical inference] These are concrete reasons
not to label subsequent approval status random or unaffected by city growth.
The text's relative date expressions are not assigned the2009 URL year as
historical completion dates.

The [Wuhan original decision](https://www.wuhan.gov.cn/zwgk/xxgk/zfwj/szfwj/202003/t20200316_973445.shtml)
was read in full including all11 area groups in its49-zone embedded list.
武政〔2004〕22号 is signed1April2004 and separately published26April.
Entries distinguish 撤消 from 摘牌. The body calls for continued management
of completed projects and coordination of projects under construction;
unused expropriated land is to be recovered. Designation loss therefore
cannot silently become shutdown of every resident firm. This local list
demonstrates recoverable records exist; it does not identify the paper's53
treated cities or prove nationwide roster completeness. One transcribed
Hongshan name is garbled on the page; no normalized name was guessed.

The publisher article-page link inventory supplies the paper PDF but no
identified supplement/data link in the inspected response. Targeted title
and data searches did not recover the170-city roster,53/117 membership or
approval cutoff. The [Xiamen2016 seminar page](https://finance.xmu.edu.cn/info/1010/54603.htm)
was read at its title, abstract and25May2016 event details. Its linked slides
route was inaccessible to the web tool; no slides or working-paper body were
inspected. A talk abstract cannot establish the2019 paper's coding.

The remaining blocker is the encodable paper-specific classification:
which approval dates and zone histories define membership, their cutoff,
historical city join and exact post indicator. Recover an author treatment
table/code or a documented historical crosswalk before retrying. Do not
require proof that every firm shut down: the paper uses an approval proxy,
whose meaning must remain explicit. Official context is now supported, but
substituting a newly invented closure treatment would change the application
rather than complete it. Existing2006 approval records remain untouched.

### Development-zone cleanup: an approval proxy, not a closure roster — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-9325f7f5f326` retains Deng Huihui, Yu Yihua and Zhao Jialing,
*Are Place-Based Policies Effective? Evidence from China's Development Zones*,
Journal of Finance and Economics45(1),4-18(2019),
[DOI10.16538/j.cnki.jfe.2019.01.001](https://doi.org/10.16538/j.cnki.jfe.2019.01.001).
The [publisher HTML](https://qks.sufe.edu.cn/mv_html/j00001/201901/6d019283-2058-40f2-9d2b-c8806c9bf9ac_WEB.htm)
was inspected in SectionsII-V, Equations1-2, Tables1-10 and footnotes2-3.
The [published PDF](https://qks.sufe.edu.cn/j/PDFFull/A181227000012.pdf)
was inspected at PDFpages4-6/printed7-9 and PDFpage14/printed17 references;
PDFpage6/printed9 was also visually checked for the group definition and
footnote. No paper file was retained.

The useful finding is a mismatch between the institutional event and the
observable exposure. SectionIII(2) explicitly substitutes post2003 provincial
zone approvals for unavailable subprovincial closure histories. Its53 treated
cities have no new provincial zones;117 comparison cities have new approvals.
The authors interpret this contrast as differential cleanup exposure. That
interpretation is reported, not a verified closure assignment. Footnote3 says
80% of new provincial approvals upgrade earlier lower-level zones: approval
need not mean the beginning of economic activity. The2018 catalogue and a
then-current zone website are retrospective sources; the approval-window
cutoff, historical names, upgrades and survivor selection need resolution.

The sample is170 cities over2000-2015 (2720 city-year observations), selected
from285 cities after excluding the four direct-administered municipalities.
The inspected text does not enumerate the170 or53 cities. Equation1 reports
city/year effects; regression tables also show province-specific time trends.
The variable named post2003 is justified by main cleanup completion in late
2004, but the wording does not settle inclusion of2004. Do not invent that
coding or conflate national2003 initiation,2003-2006 implementation and the
paper's post indicator. City-year outcomes come from city statistical
yearbooks; exposure requires a dated zone-to-city crosswalk.

[Analytical inference] Matching balance in Table2 does not establish parallel
outcome trends. Table5's alternative event dates are not a conventional
lead/lag event study. Later approval-based grouping can select cities on
subsequent development. The200/300/400km agglomeration splits in Table9 are
heterogeneity analyses, not legal assignment thresholds or an RD. These
limitations should survive any future canonical admission rather than be
erased by the paper's quasi-experiment label.

Resolve the actual approval classification and its institutional meaning
before publication. Compare the existing2006 provincial-zone village
exposure and industrial-land area-churn records: their cleanup background
does not establish a separate direct-revocation case, and a new outcome or
proxy alone does not create another mechanism. Reference31 points to Chen,
Lu and Xiang(2016), *Geography versus policy*, as a working paper; its body
and publication status have not been inspected. This is a follow-up lead,
not corroborating evidence. No canonical record or ready increment results
from this screen.

### Green-credit resolution: customer risk is not a blanket industry ban — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-f7a78ac49fb4` follows `candidate-1a22107b9705`. New primary
inspection closes the regulatory boundary and clarifies the policy clock,
but does not recover the paper's actual industry-to-firm treatment mapping.

The [State Council Gazette text](http://www.gov.cn/gongbao/content/2012/content_2163593.htm)
was read from 银监发〔2012〕4号 through Article30 via direct HTTP. It signs
the notice on29January2012; Article29 makes effectiveness depend on publication.
Articles10-11 require differentiated, dynamic industry/customer environmental
and social-risk assessment. Article17 bars noncompliant customers, while
Article19 permits suspension/termination of disbursement when major risks
arise. These are not a statutory rule denying every borrower in16 named
industries or a guaranteed loan-maturity reduction. Article2 defines covered
banking institutions and Article29 extends reference application to specified
other institutions. Bank-level assessment, compliance and supervision are
part of the implementation regime, not random treatment.

The [MIIT contemporary announcement](https://www.miit.gov.cn/jgsj/jns/gzdt/art/2020/art_00f653644bab40ee9c4eff7c20e854a3.html),
dated28February2012, was inspected in full. It identifies24February as the
CBRC press conference/seminar attended by MIIT, establishing public launch
by that date. Its migrated2020 URL is not enactment timing. It does not prove
that every bank first changed credit decisions on that day. January signature,
February public launch, paper-coded annual post2012 and operational response
remain distinct. For this annual application, month-level response uncertainty
is a timing limitation rather than the remaining core blocker. Shanghai's
reprint signature discrepancy is not silently corrected by choosing whichever
date seems convenient.

The [MEP14September2010 consultation announcement](https://www.mee.gov.cn/gkml/sthjbgw/qt/201009/t20100914_194484.htm)
was read in full. It confirms consultation, a25September comment deadline
and a16-sector disclosure proposal, not an enacted2012 credit list. The
[eight-page consultation attachment reproduction](https://www.chinaacc.com/upload/html/2010/9/15/sunxia520820109159215316576.pdf)
was read in full as a reproduced institutional document, not as an official
hosted final rule. Article15 names thermal power, steel, cement, electrolytic
aluminium, coal, metallurgy, chemicals, petrochemicals, building materials,
paper, brewing, pharmaceuticals, fermentation, textiles, leather and mining.
It directs detailed classification to 环办函〔2008〕373号. The SDUFE notice
mirror was read, but its attachment link contains an unresolved `{imgpath}`
and returns404. The CAEP attachment had a certificate-hostname mismatch via
HTTPS and404 via HTTP; that copy was not inspected.

The [original MEP2008 classification notice and its full embedded table](https://www.mee.gov.cn/gkml/hbb/bgth/200910/t20091022_174891.htm)
were inspected, including all14 top-level categories and the light-industry
subcategories. They define production processes rather than CSMAR/CSRC
numeric codes: metal surface treatment can belong, while an undifferentiated
manufacturing label is insufficient. Brewing, paper and fermentation are
nested under light industry; its additional sugar/vegetable-oil rows show why
mechanically equating the14-table structure with the16-name consultation
list can alter the sample. The document concerns listing/refinancing
environmental review, not a new green-credit assignment in2008.

An inspected [2012 adjustment notice](https://www.mee.gov.cn/gkml/hbb/bwj/201211/t20121129_242908.htm),
body SectionsI-V and final clause, changes environmental-review procedures
and disclosure obligations; its8October signature is distinct from the
29November webpage date. Its application/report attachment was not read.
This is a documented same-year financing/regulatory overlap, not evidence
that the credit-policy coefficient must be zero or that another canonical
case can be admitted. The [2016 repeal decision](https://www.mee.gov.cn/gkml/hbb/bl/201607/t20160715_360790.htm),
notice and AppendixII item38 inspected, explicitly repeals the2008 listing
classification. That is outside the paper's2008-2015 window; it limits
claims of current legal validity, not use of a fixed historical exposure.

The published paper's page7 Tables2-3 were additionally inspected: periods
are2008-2011 and2012-2015; baseline7,138 observations and industry/year
effects are explicit. A text search of the16-page paper found no clustering
specification, so its reported t-statistics must not be labeled industry-
clustered. The exact industry code vintage, mapping of process-level categories
to listed firms, and whether industry membership is frozen before treatment
remain unrecovered. No supplementary file or code inventory was inspected;
the publisher page returned403. A full list of official sector names does
not close this mapping merely by resembling standard codes.

Disposition: retain **blocked in the candidate layer**, without a new canonical
or ready count. The useful next evidence is a reviewable treatment crosswalk,
supplement/code, or a well-documented application recovering that mapping,
not more general policy descriptions. Compare any recovered mapping with
the existing JDE punishment-history application before choosing one policy
case with multiple applications versus genuinely distinct mechanisms. Do not
count a different proxy, outcome or pollution-index robustness as a new
institutional variation. Original rules and existing knowledge remain intact.

### Green-credit industry exposure: published application screen — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-14857ec16ef9` covers Yao, Pan, Sensoy, Uddin and Cheng (2021),
*Green credit policy and firm performance: What we learn from China*,
Energy Economics101,105415,
[DOI10.1016/j.eneco.2021.105415](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eneco.2021.105415).
This is one source in the existing firm/environment journal lane, not another
completed journal audit. The [coauthor's university-hosted published PDF](https://yoksis.bilkent.edu.tr/pdf/files/15548.pdf)
has16 pages. Pages1-5,12,14-15 were inspected for identity, institutional account,
sample, model, robustness construction and variable definitions; Table1 on
page6 was inspected. Page5 was also rendered to verify Eq.(1) and the two-column
reading order. Other pages were located or partially extracted, not claimed
as a complete appendix audit. No PDF was stored in this repository.

The paper actually estimates an industry-exposure contrast around2012, not a
city pilot or firm-level random loan allocation. Sections4.1-4.4 identify
CSMAR A-share firms in2008-2015, excluding financial/ST firms and missing
variables, and winsorizing continuous variables at1/99 percentiles. Treatment
uses16 heavily polluting industries attributed to the2010 environmental
disclosure consultation draft; other sample industries form the comparison.
`Post` equals one for2012-2015 and zero for2008-2011. Eq.(1) includes the
interaction, separate treatment/post terms, contemporaneous controls, industry
and year effects; it is not the firm-FE baseline that a reader might assume.
Table1 reports7,138 observations, not7,138 firms. AppendixA defines ROA,
ROE and Tobin'sQ and confirms the post window. The practical joins are firm
identifier/year plus a versioned industry-to-exposure crosswalk, not a list
of city designation dates or a direct observation of denied loans.

Section7.3 explicitly says the draft's sectors do not completely match the
industry classification. Its alternative uses2011 industry pollution per
output, four pollutants (SO2, soot/dust, solid waste and wastewater),
standardization and aggregation, then an index cutoff of0.1669. This is an
alternative measurement of the same2012 credit-policy exposure, not a second
variation counted for another result. Recover the exact baseline16-sector
crosswalk and classification vintage; if the alternative is used, recover
its source table and standardization rather than silently correcting the
printed Eq.(9) index inconsistency. Section5.1's isolated2014 policy reference
conflicts with Sections4.3 and AppendixA's2012 coding. Table13 and its caption
also distinguish firm/year additive effects from industry-year/province-year
variants, despite wording that calls additive effects 'firm-year'. These
are source interpretation limits, not reasons to invent a different rollout.

Reported pretrend, matching and placebo checks do not establish exchangeable
industries, random enforcement, or a causal mediation effect. Contemporaneous
controls can absorb channels, and other environmental/industry shocks can
coincide with2012. Financial constraints and investment are downstream
mechanism exercises, not independently randomized instruments. Clustering and
the precise firm industry assignment require further inspection; no inference
correction or replication result is claimed at screen.

Retain as a `china-variation` candidate for identity resolution and primary
grounding. Compare with existing blocked candidate `candidate-b14409ff7659`
(JDE2021): it uses punishment history and bank loans, not this industry proxy.
Different empirical proxies or outcomes alone do not prove distinct statutory
assignment; resolve whether they belong in one policy case with separate
applications before creating canonical records. Reuse the existing Gazette
lead for 银监发〔2012〕4号, but re-inspect relevant provisions and publication
clock. The industry's2010 consultation classification is a measurement source,
not automatically a binding2012 credit eligibility rule. The inaccessible
JCF2023 paper remains separately blocked and supplies no substantive evidence.
Screen publishes no canonical record and adds no ready count.

### Innovative-city resolution: approval, launch and reporting are different clocks — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-a4650292565d` follows `candidate-636b8a0cf760`. The previous
screen's2009/2010 discrepancy is now more specific; it does not prove that
the paper simply used a wrong year. New primary evidence identifies an earlier
approval event as well as a later public launch/reporting event. Neither date
can be substituted for the paper's absent city-year treatment table by intuition.

The [Guangzhou science bureau's official reproduction](https://kjj.gz.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfg/zcfgwj/content/post_2627261.html)
of 国科发体〔2010〕155号 includes the previously unread guidance attachment.
The notice and guidance SectionsI-IV were read in full. SectionII explicitly
selects places with stronger innovation foundations, economic/social development
and surrounding-area influence. SectionIV(2) specifies provincial government
recommendation followed by MOST decision and approval, then a local implementation
plan; provincial pilots are a separate supported lane. SectionIV(3) gives
priority support to eligible tasks, platforms and talent through existing channels,
not an automatic equal grant to every designated city. Monitoring entails annual
self-reports. Local differentiation and city/district coverage are explicit.
The signed April6,2010 notice governs dating; this mirror's April5 webpage
timestamp does not establish earlier enactment. Its monitoring DOC was not read.

The [MOST2010 annual-report chronology excerpt](https://most.gov.cn/xxgk/xinxifenlei/fdzdgknr/zfwzndbb/201902/P020101015393388438419.pdf)
contains six pages, printed180-185. The first page labels the chronology2009;
the final page was read and visually inspected. Under December10 it records
MOST's reply approving Haidian and19 other cities/districts as national innovative
pilots. The PDF headers are not completely consistent about report year, so
the chronology label, month grouping and event text were checked together.
This establishes an official record of a December2009 approval event, not the
names or effective dates of every one of those20 jurisdictions.

The [Shanghai2010 executive-meeting record](https://www.shanghai.gov.cn/nw45115/20200824/0001-45115_1391549.html),
dated July27 with meeting July26, was read in full. It describes Yangpu as one
of20 MOST pilots determined in January2010 and places local knowledge-innovation
work as early as2003. Thus a2009 reply,2010 selection/public launch description,
and pre-existing local program can coexist. The specific original approval letter
and paper coding are needed before choosing the operative exposure clock. Do
not reinterpret Shanghai-wide data as Yangpu-only outcomes or use its2015 plan
target as a treatment commencement date.

The inspected NDRC2010 notice's16 approved city plans, MOST's20-city/district
event, and the paper's15 additional2009 cities remain different enumerations.
The2016 stock list helps recover scope but cannot supply historical dates.
Searches of the original-journal host, the exact Chinese title and contemporaneous
agency notices did not recover the original2019 article or the47-sample-city
coding table. Search results and citations to other papers are not substitutes.
The coauthor's inspected institutional bibliography remains sufficient to retain
the Economic Perspectives2019 lead without claiming it was published in Economic
Research Journal; original Chinese text is a follow-up route, not an additional
arbitrary requirement for every translated paper.

**Resolution outcome.** Leave the candidate blocked at the serving boundary.
The institution and selective assignment are now better grounded, but one
paper-used regime and encodable exposure map are not yet closed. A subsequent
task should obtain the study's47 treated/222 comparison city roster and coded
start years, reconcile MOST versus NDRC approvals (including overlapping places),
and state how district pilots inherit or do not inherit treatment at prefecture
scale. City-year policy approval can be a useful intention-to-treat exposure;
it need not prove implementation of every local subsidy. The missing step here
is identifying which approval and spatial unit the study actually uses, not
requiring perfect compliance or a full independent replication. Read the source's
actual comparison before replacing its exposure with a cleaner-looking policy.
If an accessible new application closes that bridge under a coherent regime,
retain it with its own evidence and reconcile this candidate rather than forcing
the older study's sample into it. No canonical or ready increment is produced.

### Innovative-city pilot: one Chinese-journal translation screen — 2026-10-04

Bounded source expansion: inspect one urban-innovation application from the
Chinese economics literature, using the author institution and a lawful English
translation to establish its origin. This is not a ranking or complete archive
audit of *Economic Perspectives* or *China Political Economy*. Screen
`task-6dc80dbae1bd` covers Li and Yang, *Has the innovative city pilot policy
improved the level of urban innovation?*, CPE4(1),2021,56-85,
DOI `10.1108/CPE-07-2021-0010`. The
[EconStor deposit](https://www.econstor.eu/bitstream/10419/319437/1/179410397X.pdf)
contains repository metadata plus the30-page published translation, licensed
CC BY4.0. It was read in memory, not stored in this repository. PDF1-2,6-16,
28 and31 were inspected; Eq1 and treatment definitions on PDF10/printed64
were visually inspected. Mechanism prose on PDF8-9 is theoretical reasoning,
not independent evidence of actual implementation or mechanism effects.

The English article's copyright line identifies *Economic Perspectives*, but
its footnote calls the original journal *Economic Research Journal*. The
[coauthor's Jilin University publication list](https://jjxy.jlu.edu.cn/info/1233/16028.htm),
representative paper9, explicitly lists 李政、杨思莹,
《创新型城市试点提升城市创新水平了吗?》,《经济学动态》,2019(8).
Thus the inspected institutional evidence supports *Economic Perspectives*,
not a claim of publication in《经济研究》. Recover the original issue/article
for bibliographic reconciliation; do not upgrade its prestige using the
translation's inconsistent footnote. The CPE DOI remains the identity of the
actual text inspected, not a fabricated DOI for the Chinese original.

**Actual use.** Section3.1 codes each pilot city as treated from its introduction
year onward and estimates city/year fixed-effects DID on log innovation.
Section3.2 uses269 cities,2003-2016:47 retained pilots and222 comparisons.
It reports57 designations during2008-2013, while background discussion refers
to61 through2016; these are not the same sample count. Cities with substantial
missing data and17 cities designated in2018 are excluded. Innovation is the
patent-value-based index from the2017 China urban/industrial innovation report,
not simply patent counts. EPS supplies controls; a Patentcloud-based per-million
population outcome is a separate robustness application. Recover stable city-code,
year and administrative-boundary joins before assuming these sources align.
No complete city-designation-year table or replication code was inspected.

Section4.2 explicitly acknowledges selection of economically stronger and more
innovative cities. PSM-DID and cohort-restricted comparisons are reported checks,
not a source of randomized assignment. The867 matched observations must not
be called867 cities. The2003-2007 pooled pretrend test is not a cohort-specific
event study, and Note2 says the trend figure is omitted and available from the
author. The inspected main regression table does not establish city-clustered
inference. Contemporary controls can themselves respond to designation;
staggered TWFE, spillovers, co-policies and outcome construction need explicit
assessment. Neither matching balance nor an insignificant pretrend certifies
parallel trends. The reported mediation regressions likewise do not independently
identify the policy's transmission channels.

**Primary-source bridge.** The
[NDRC original notice](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/tz/201001/t20100111_964592.html),
发改高技〔2010〕30号, signed January6,2010 and published January11,
was read in full. SectionI approves16 named cities' submitted overall plans;
SectionIV requires detailed planning and implementation when conditions permit.
Approval therefore is not a verified simultaneous funding or operational start.
This cannot silently establish the translation's statement that15 cities,
including Dalian and Qingdao, entered in2009. The
[MOST notice](https://www.most.gov.cn/xxgk/xinxifenlei/fdzdgknr/fgzc/gfxwj/gfxwj2010before/201004/t20100419_76811.html),
国科发体〔2010〕155号, verifies April6 signature and April16 publication,

### Registered-capital resolution: statutory scope and a revised application — 2026-10-04

The [December31,2023 AEA manuscript](https://www.aeaweb.org/conference/2024/program/paper/aHEYkQGn)
is a newly inspected67-page version. PDF1,6-8,13-18,32,34,37-40 and50
were read; the entry equation, definitions and footnotes on PDF38/printed37
were also visually inspected. Section4.1 now reports annual tax data2012-2016,
an unnamed province, March2013-December2014 registrants with positive2014
assets/revenue, and weekend/holiday exclusions. Section6.1 replaces the
old entry DiRD with firm-type DID: private LLCs versus state-, foreign- and
HMT-owned enterprises, with ULEs examined separately. It states post starts
in2014Q1; Table9 describes quarterly log entry counts. Text instead labels

not the old6.7%. The RDiT still uses March1,2014 registration; Table6 reports
industry-month clustering and triangular kernels, while Section4.2 describes
rectangular baseline kernels. These passages require version-specific coding
reconciliation, not silent correction. UBC's forthcoming2026 deposit remains
inaccessible; this AEA version is not certified as the final journal text.

Primary law materially improves the institutional account. The
[original amendment reprinted by the environment ministry](https://jjjcz.mee.gov.cn/djfg/gjflfg/fl/201312/t20131201_444480.html),
SectionVII and final effective-date paragraph, removes the general contribution
restrictions and preserves special statutory regimes; Company Law changes
take effect March1,2014. Its webpage's December1 date is inconsistent with
the enactment date, so do not use that timestamp as announcement evidence.
The [JETRO reproduction of Presidential Order8](https://www.jetro.go.jp/ext_images/world/asia/cn/law/pdf/invest_064_cn.pdf),
PDF1-3 text inspected, identifies enactment December28,2013 and effectiveness
March1,2014. This is a reproduced legal document, not evidence that every
registration office first implemented the change on that date.

The [pre-reform law on Shenzhen's government site](https://www.sz.gov.cn/cn/xxgk/zfxxgj/zcfg/content/post_8979947.html),
Articles26-30 and58-60, was inspected as historical law, not the2023 revision.
Ordinary LLCs faced a20% first contribution, a CNY30,000 minimum, and generally
two years to complete contributions; investment companies had five years.
Article59 required one-person LLC shareholders to pay their charter contribution
in full at once, with a CNY100,000 minimum. This is not merely the ordinary
20% rule with a higher floor, and includes corporate sole shareholders as well
as individuals. The paper's compressed discussion cannot replace this distinction.

The [State Council plan in Liaoning's official gazette](https://www.ln.gov.cn/web/zwgkx/lnsrmzfgb/2014n/qk/2014n_dqq/gwywj/573DE2C3C46B461793700669B615A6C3/index.shtml),
国发〔2014〕7号, dated February7,2014, SectionsII-IV and the27-category
appendix were inspected. General subscription registration removed minimums,
fixed contribution timing, cash-share restrictions and registration verification,
but retained specified paid-in regimes. Partnerships and sole-proprietor entities
already under declaration/subscription continued their existing rules. Charter
contribution duties and public disclosure remained. Annual-report, address and
electronic-registration changes belonged to a broader reform package; their
authorization is not proof of simultaneous local implementation. The plan
expressly makes a provincial disclosure system a prerequisite for implementation.

Early local exposure is established, not hypothetical. The
[Hunan government opinion](https://hunan.gov.cn/hnszf/szf/hnzb_18/2014_18/2014nd3q_18/szfbgtwj_98287_18/201403/t20140310_4701089.html),
湘政办发〔2013〕82号, dated December31,2013, SectionsI(3),II(1)-(2),
states January1,2014 implementation. The
[original Hunan implementing rules](https://www.hunan.gov.cn/xxgk/wjk/szbmwj/201312/t20131229_5259623.html),
湘工商注字〔2013〕363号, Articles7-14,23 and38-42, separately confirm
January1 effectiveness, retained exceptions, charter contribution obligations
and adjustment when amended national law takes effect. The page metadata date
and signed notice date differ; Article42 fixes the effective date. Hunan is
not asserted to be the anonymous study province. No new Hunan canonical is
created: an inspected paper application to that implementation is still needed.

**Decision and remaining bridge.** Retain the candidate, but block canonical
admission in this bounded task. National legal identity is substantially clearer;
the unresolved object is the paper-used exposure and population. Obtain an
inspectable final supplement/code or an author explanation of the anonymous
province's actual cutoff, the51 organizational-type mappings, sector exclusions,
and the2014Q1 versus March1 entry clock. Confidentiality need not be breached:
confirmation that the sample jurisdiction was not already reformed would suffice
for timing, without publishing its identity. This is not a demand to independently
replicate every coefficient. It is the minimum bridge that tells a new reader
which observations change institutional exposure and which only represent the
authors' behavioral control assumption. State/foreign ownership does not legally
exempt a company from the general law; the paper's argument is weaker financial
constraint, so its controls are not verified statutory non-treatment. Positive
outcome selection and missing getihu also limit entry/composition interpretation.
Do not convert the primary Hunan rule into a guessed study cutoff, blend the
two manuscript designs, or split them into additional ready cases merely because
the estimator changed. No canonical or ready increment is produced.

minus retained earnings. Zero-inclusive logs and IHS profits have distinct
interpretations. Age at outcome measurement, tax-return selection and
registration-versus-operation differences matter. The source is a

province: registration information through mid2017 and annual returns/
financial statements2010-2016, covering smaller firms absent from ASIF.

as a public download. A lawful comparable dataset and stable taxpayer-ID
join are conditions for reuse.

AppendixA uses previous-year discontinuities around December28 to assess
anticipation, with63-day windows and business-date clustered errors.
AppendixB's entry counts use differences-in-discontinuities around March1
in2014 versus2013 because the placebo year itself exhibits a date jump.
These require stability of seasonal discontinuities across years; they do
not prove no strategic delay or no other March2014 shock. The appendix
reports6.7% aggregate entry growth in this version, whereas the2026
institutional abstract reports33%. Do not blend the versions or attribute
old estimates to the forthcoming journal article. Holiday exclusions,
retroactive relief for existing firms, manipulation and bundled reforms

### Xiong'an resolution: announcement, planning and entrustment are different clocks — 2026-10-04

Resolve `task-71ff6b747bda` follows `candidate-8bcf418c9bab`.

ChapterI Section2, explicitly names the four towns in the1770km2 planning
scope. Selected fiscal, implementation and adjacent-area-control passages
were also inspected. Planning coverage is not a town-by-town administrative
effective-date register. The2018 publication must not be backdated to
April2017, and adjacent-area controls themselves make a strictly untreated
outside ring an assumption to test, not an institutional fact.

Three further original-authority passages establish that entrustment cannot
be inferred from today's county names. The [Hebei archaeology institute's
2023 report](https://www.hbswwkg.com/2023-02/18/content_8950705.html),
section on Xiong County's ancient-city site, explicitly places Maozhou's

report](https://www.xiongan.gov.cn/2018-11/06/c_129987384.htm) identifies
the three former Renqiu towns as entrusted and reports a road upgrade.
