places Longhua residents'2019 insurance participation in Anxin under
冀人社字〔2018〕297号; it does not establish Longhua's exact administrative
handover day or the start of environmental enforcement. Secondary sources'
April20,2018 date for Longhua remains a retrieval lead, not a verified date.

Additional final-paper passages were inspected at pages12,19-21,23,27,
29-30. Local DIRD bandwidths in TableB.3 are20/15/10/5km. TableB.4 uses
changes in raw PM2.5, not logged annual PM2.5; its local quadratic and
linear versions have different kernels/inference. Fig.A.3 defines positive
signed distance as inside. The500 spatially contiguous33-town placebo
clusters and Jifu runner-up comparison in pages12/23/TableB.5 are not
randomization of the real designation. Page19 says data are available on
request, not a public replication package. Its null nightlight coefficient
does not establish zero economic cost or a growth benefit. Balance TableB.1
is conditional on spatial polynomials; non-significance does not certify
exchangeability. Appendix figures were located/read through their captions;
their plotted curves have not been visually audited in this resolve pass.

The official planning footprint broadly agrees with the paper's named
treated areas, but the historical exposure clock remains decision-critical.
The inspected paper uses a static33-town treatment indicator with a2017
policy interaction and does not reconcile four-town entrustment in2018.
This is not, by itself, proof that an announcement-exposure estimand is
invalid: expectations could precede handover. Nor is it proof that all33
towns faced the same implemented governance in2017. No inspected source
closes that distinction, supplies the exact dated33-town polygon, or tests
excluding/re-timing the added towns. The nine-control versus twelve-neighbor
wording and `Baozhou` spelling remain unresolved sample-description issues.
Do not substitute a three-county sample the paper did not report, or create
a new2018 application solely from plausible design logic.

Keep the candidate blocked, with no new canonical or ready increment.
Reopen on an inspectable original four-town entrustment/implementation
register plus the authors' treatment polygon/code or an explicit sample
construction explanation resolving which clock the interaction measures.
The sources and verified differences above make that follow-up narrower;
they are not grounds for abandoning the otherwise relevant source.

then read in separate bounded outputs. Page1 confirms April10,2026 online
publication and the2026 issue; neither is a policy date.

The retained mainland-China exposure is the creation of Xiong'an New Area,
paper-reported April1,2017, with a territorial policy bundle rather than
an isolated environmental-rule shock. The authors compare0.01-degree
PM2.5 grid cells in33 towns against126 towns in nine adjacent counties
over2013-2020. Page4 says29 treated towns belong to Xiong County,
Rongcheng and Anxin and four to surrounding areas: Maozhou, Gougezhuang,
Qijianfang and Longhua. Do not equate this static sample boundary with
the three-county announcement or assume the four towns were administered
by the new area from the same date. Page5 footnote5 names nine controls:

and the spelling `Baozhou`. Administrative spelling and historical units
need verification. Page8's phrase about12 immediate neighboring counties
does not agree with the stated nine control counties; reconcile it against
the sample files rather than inventing three controls.

The preferred difference-in-discontinuities model on pages7-8 uses
`Treat town x After`, grid and year effects, third-order latitude/longitude
polynomials interacted with the post dummy, town weather and year-specific
distance-to-Beijing/Tianjin controls. The alternative uses side-specific
linear distance to the boundary; preferred fits use the full regional
sample, not an automatically optimal narrow RD bandwidth. Table2 has
71,472 grid-year observations and reports the preferred coefficient
-0.021. Town-clustered inference and20km spatial/five-year serial
correlation corrections are reported. Selection by location/resources and
pre-policy pollution differences are explicitly visible; conditional
balance and insignificant pretrends do not prove random allocation.
The estimand concerns the new-area bundle, which includes financial
transfers, institution relocation, environmental governance and industrial
restructuring. It is not a separately identified effect of each channel
or a health outcome measured in this sample.

The inspected data passages identify ACAG satellite annual PM2.5,
ERA5-Land town-aggregated weather, grid-centroid geographic joins, EOG
nightlights, LandScan population and MODIS vegetation. County fiscal
accounts cover2015-2020; Qichacha supplies registration/investment and
innovation records, and landchina supplies land transactions. Grid-,
town- and county-level applications must retain separate data requirements.
Qichacha access and town geocoding are conditions, not freely available
replication assets. Agricultural burning is one mechanism check within a
regional-development/environment study, not its central assignment.

Pages10-11 inspect exclusion of wind directions and neighboring control
towns/5km/10km rings, and controls for Air10,2+26 and the Baoding/Langfang
No-Coal-Zone policy. These are tests of specific confounds, not proof that
controls never receive spillovers or differential interventions. Retain
as `china-variation`, distinct provisionally from the Beijing-origin NCFR
candidate: assignment here is destination new-area jurisdiction, not
Beijing relief eligibility. Resolve must inspect original establishment
and planning/entrustment sources, recover the33-town polygon and four-town

### Provincial river-exit exposure and industrial location — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-73a7ce5984e9` inspects Cai, Hongbin, Yuyu Chen and Qing Gong,
*Polluting thy neighbor: Unintended consequences of China's pollution
reduction mandates*, JEEM76(2016):86-104,
DOI10.1016/j.jeem.2015.01.002, in the existing JEEM lane. The
[institution-hosted final19-page PDF](https://economics.sas.upenn.edu/system/files/2018-03/Polluting.thy_.neighbor.JEEM_.2016.03_0.pdf)
was read in memory: first-page identity, institutional and empirical/data
sections printed88-93, Tables2-3 printed95-96, selected mechanism passages
printed97-99, and temporal test/conclusion/references printed100-104.
The printed91 regression and dummy definitions were visually inspected.
Separate online supplementary TablesA1-A2 and any replication package were
not inspected. The PDF confirms the2016 issue and January2015 online date;
neither date is policy onset.

The actual baseline is not a simple2001 policy DID. Its three dimensions
are water-polluting industry `POL`, upstream-province side `DOWN`, and
riverside `RIV`. For one river crossing a province boundary, A is the last
riverside county in the upstream province, B the first in the next province,
and a/b are same-province, non-riverside neighboring border counties.
`DOWN=1` for A and a even though a is not itself on the river; `RIV=1` for
A and B. The contrast is the polluting/non-polluting difference in A versus B
minus the analogous difference in a versus b. It is not a station-distance
RD, a randomly selected lax-enforcement county, or a government list
assigning permits to firms. The paper interprets this geography as exposure
to discretionary provincial enforcement and downstream spillover incentives.

Data section printed92 gives24 major rivers,232 counties,58 four-county
groups and30 two-digit manufacturing industries. The baseline2001-2008
panel has33,312 county-industry-year observations; the new-entry sample
in Table3 has17,592. Mining is excluded, and an industry is retained within
a group if present in at least one constituent county. ASIF1998-2008
supports production/firm counts;2008 missing value added is reconstructed
from VAT due using17%, which can confound activity measurement with tax
reporting. County controls come from China Regional Yearbook and highway
maps, with province GDP deflators. Food-processing inclusion is industrial,
not an agriculture-centered research question. New-firm classification,
historical county/industry joins and zero/log treatment still require exact
reconstruction, rather than assuming every missing cell is zero.

The baseline includes group-by-year and industry-by-year effects, not an
ordinary county-year rollout model. Table3 notes county-year clustered
errors; prose describes correlation more broadly, so serial/spatial
dependence needs separate assessment. Printed101-102 adds `AFTER=1` from
2001 to form a four-way interaction over1998-2008, with45,449 observations
for value added/firm counts and22,998 for entry. The post interaction is
not statistically significant for the first two outcomes in Table7; the
entry interaction is significant. Do not turn its abstract's causal framing
or baseline18.4 log-point estimate into a proven20% policy-onset effect.

The paper reports a2001 national target,2002 decomposition, later2006-2007
strengthening and decentralized enforcement. These reports need primary
verification, particularly the target-document identity, discharge versus
water-quality metrics, and the claimed cadre consequences. The2004
fee-per-firm mechanism check is a separate cross-sectional proxy, not a
measured statutory difference in county tax rates or a water-quality outcome.
Manufacturing location and provincial boundaries can select exposure;
transport access, industry trends, changing reporting and later policy
changes can also reproduce the contrast.

Retain a `china-variation` candidate for resolving the provincial river-exit
enforcement mechanism, original targets and county-group construction.
`china-water-quality-monitoring-rd` uses firm position relative to monitors,
while `china-2007-pollution-cadre-accountability-subsidy-exposure` uses
pre-reform subsidies and a later national reform. Those related cases do
not cover this actual geographical contrast. No canonical is changed by
this screen; the24-river/group inventory and industry appendix remain
specific next sources, not a license to invent a treatment roster.

### Meningococcal vaccination and childhood exposure — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-2769fbd1ee17` retains one source in the existing bounded
*China Economic Quarterly* lane, not a journal-ranking claim or archive sweep.
何凡, 王姣 and 黄炜, *疫苗接种、人力资本积累及其经济收益*,
2025,25(6):1478-1494, DOI10.13821/j.cnki.ceq.2025.06.04, is relevant to
Mainland Chinese human-capital formation and adult economic outcomes.
The [official article](https://nsd.pku.edu.cn/docs/2025-12/df88982f26d7435faa814f32a73ebd0d.pdf)
was rendered and read in memory: PDF pages1,4-8 (printed1478,1481-1485)
establish identity, institutional account, equation1 and the data construction.
The [official appendix](https://nsd.pku.edu.cn/docs/2025-12/ecd2d93f3e4f4b0fb4b8e145b1d9e14a.pdf),
PDF pages1-5 (contents and printed1-4), documents confounder controls and
alternative exposure definitions. No paper file was stored; uninspected
sections are not claimed as recovered.

The paper interacts pre-vaccine city meningococcal incidence with age at
recorded local vaccine introduction: ages0-15 are exposed, ages16-25 form
the comparison. Local dates in the CFPS sample span1979-1986; where a
chronicle does not state introduction, the earliest vaccination entry is
substituted. This is not observed individual uptake or random vaccination.
The severity variable averages available epidemic-year case counts from
1966-1968 and1976-1978, divided by1982 census city population, rather than
each year's population; missing years are not coded as zero. The authors
collected disease accounts for246 cities and matched90 cities to childhood
locations in CFPS2010, with historical boundary adjustments. Their8519-person
sample excludes specified residential movers and missing core variables.
Equation1 uses city and province-by-birth-year fixed effects, city-clustered
errors, sex, ethnicity and hukou at age3. Individual outcomes come from
CFPS2010; the1982 census supplies the historical disease-rate denominator,
not a separate adult-outcome application.

Resolution must verify the national authorization and local implementation
boundary, recover the city-date provenance and earliest-entry substitutions,
and specify the historical-city joins and exposure units. Pre-vaccine disease
severity and local timing are not assumed independent of development or
migration. Appendix I addresses Cultural Revolution school closure/mobility,
famine, other disease campaigns and city cohort trends; these checks do not
prove absence of confounding. Appendix II's common1980 date and actual1980-city
restriction are sensitivities, not evidence that all cities began in1980.
Its disease-decline measure uses post-treatment incidence and must not replace
the baseline predetermined severity. Herd immunity, selective survival,
residential selection and heterogeneous cohort effects limit interpretation.
Retain as `china-variation` for resolution; canonical unchanged.

Resolve `task-4053ef680095` inspected main printed1480-1481 and1486,
including the national disease history, vaccine stages, footnotes and dynamic
cohort plots. Printed1481 distinguishes1975 experimental application in five
provinces,1980 national product approval, epidemic-driven local expansion
and repeated dosing; its Tianjin1979 example remains a paper-reported claim.
Printed1486 reports pre-cohort comparisons without identifying an underlying
random assignment. The outcome source was reread at higher resolution:
CFPS2010 supplies individual outcomes, while1982 census population supplies
the disease-rate denominator. Do not turn a denominator source into an
independent outcome dataset.

The [Shandong health-chronicle mirror](https://shandong-chorography.org/database/a/section/68/article/123/),
section IV, records expanded A-group polysaccharide use from1976 and broad
childhood vaccination after1980. The [Sishui county-chronicle mirror](https://shandong-chorography.org/database/c88/section/1/article/484/),
chapter26 section1, records December1980 initiation for children under15,
with epidemic case counts in selected years. Both pages explicitly identify
themselves as unofficial mirrors of a discontinued provincial database;
the original edition/scans were not recovered. These are useful archival
leads, not independently authenticated city treatment rows. A county date
must not be silently propagated to its entire prefecture, and under15 is
not automatically identical to the paper's inclusive0-15 age convention.
The [official Fuzhou history-office republication](https://fz.fjdsfzw.org.cn/wap/2020-03-01/content_88519.html),
March1,2020, section15, discusses Jiangsu rather than Fuzhou vaccination:
it reports the1980 vaccine naming transition and regular vaccination in
Jiangsu. It supplies historical context, not the paper's90-city date table.

The inspected publisher issue links article and appendix, but no vaccination
replication package. The NHC historical vaccine page returned HTTP412 in
both browser retrieval and direct read; its search text is not verified
evidence. Neither inspected article/appendix nor these local accounts
recover the city-date/source table, which dates are earliest-entry proxies,
or the county-to-historical-city mapping. Early trial use, approval and
mass rollout therefore remain a material measurement boundary, not merely
a missing cosmetic citation. Finish blocked without canonical admission;
reopen when the dated city source table or a directly documented matching
institutional reconstruction becomes accessible.

A related high-value follow-up is He, Huang, Pan and Wang,
*Public Health, Human Capital, and Economic Growth: The Lasting Effects of
Disease Control in China*, DOI10.1162/REST.a.1781. The
[author publication list](https://huangweipku.com/research/) identifies
*Review of Economics and Statistics* forthcoming status; the
[author institution's May16,2026 announcement](https://csyjzx.jxufe.edu.cn/news-show-768.html)
reports online publication. These are identity leads, not inspected methods.
The publisher article link was inaccessible through the web tool. Screen
its full text/appendix separately for recoverable historical source tables
and distinct disease regimes; do not assume it uses identical cohorts,
dates or estimands, or duplicate the meningococcal mechanism by paper DOI.

Resolve `task-517b17a00f3a` recovered the original institutional boundary.
The [SPC2013 authoritative release](https://www.court.gov.cn/fabu/xiangqing/5867.html)
was read in full, not merely through its indexed gazette copy. 法释〔2013〕26号
was adopted November13, signed November21, posted November29 and effective
January1,2014 (preamble/ArticleXV). ArticlesII/IV cover effective judgments
with exceptions for secrets/privacy, juvenile crime, mediation and other
unsuitable cases. ArticleVIII requires technical preparation within seven
days after effectiveness; it is not the successor's seven-working-day upload
rule. ArticleXIV explicitly lets high courts determine upload schedules for
midwestern basic courts. A national effective date therefore does not prove
synchronous local implementation. ArticlesVI/VII retain many party identities
but require specified anonymization and deletion, contrary to an unrestricted
real-name-data interpretation.

The [SPC2016 authoritative release](https://www.court.gov.cn/fabu/xiangqing/25321.html)
was also read in full. 法释〔2016〕19号, adopted July25, signed August29,
effective October1,2016, changes the document list and exceptions (III/IV),
requires publication of non-disclosure metadata with a secrecy exception (VI),
and specifies upload within seven working days after legal effectiveness
(VII). ArticlesVIII-XI alter redaction and retained identifiers; ArticleXVI
provides withdrawal/correction. These changes need their own measurement
boundary in any extended panel, not an assumption of constant disclosure
definitions after2014. The [SPC July3,2013 announcement](https://www.court.gov.cn/zixun/xiangqing/5513.html),
read in full, concerns the Supreme Court's own decisions and interim rules;
it does not establish nationwide lower-court treatment from July2013.

The gazette pages failed through redirect/timeout, but the authoritative
release pages above supplied the actual legal text. Elsevier's retrieval API
returned metadata only and non-open-access status, not the published methods.
Exact DOI/title search did not recover a public author version; ResearchGate
offers a request rather than full text. No author was contacted. Indexed
publisher previews remain leads, including inconsistent document-count
descriptions; no total, sample or threshold is adopted without methods.

The institutional instrument is now verified, but the paper-to-data assignment
bridge remains open: pre-reform years and case-date basis, per-capita
denominator, low-transparency threshold or continuous coding, geographic
aggregation, missingness/withdrawals, firm headquarters versus operating
location, comparison sample, estimator, clustering and concurrent reforms.
Legal local discretion and administrative capacity are concrete selection
concerns; neither national compulsion nor covariate balancing establishes
random exposure. Preserve the candidate blocked on full methods/appendix
access, not contested merely because the sources are unavailable. Reopen on
a lawful final or author methods source; do not repeatedly re-fetch these
closed legal points. No canonical change; ready net0, cumulative109.

Equation2 compares non-big-five commercial banks with the named big five,

insured/uninsured-bank DID. The126-bank2011–2017 sample excludes foreign
branches, policy banks and Postal Savings Bank;670 bank-year observations

not proof that other banking reforms are irrelevant.

### Farmland replacement costs and urban development — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-0f4732df2736` retains Yue Yu's *The Local and Aggregate Effects
of Land-Use Regulation on Farmland Protection*, JUE155,103908 (2026),
[DOI](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jue.2026.103908). The
[author publication page](https://sites.google.com/view/yue-yu/home) confirms
publication; publisher full text was not retrieved. This is urban development,
not an agriculture-dominant collection extension.

The [AEA-hosted May5,2021 draft](https://www.aeaweb.org/conference/2022/preliminary/paper/hYQ9y9e2)
was inspected, printed pp6-12, AppendixA.3 p63 and TableA.3 p51. It studies
city-jurisdiction GDP, urban land and census population. Exposure is the
share of steep terrain in a five-kilometre inward administrative-border
buffer, excluding existing urban land and its two-kilometre outward buffer,
interacted with post1999. This is continuous DID, not random land quotas.
Yearbooks and censuses require consistent jurisdictions; some boundary
changes remain problematic. Inference clusters by jurisdiction, with spatial
robustness. A fixed terrain measure does not eliminate differential growth
or geography-driven confounding. Welfare simulations are not reduced-form
treatment effects. Methods are draft-reported, not certified final.

Resolve must recover primary replacement law, its actual jurisdictional
boundary and exceptions, final geography/coding and historical joins, and
reconcile version changes. The existing RSUE quota-allocation candidate
`candidate-935609e71b7f` is related, not evidence that these mechanisms are
identical. Candidate `candidate-4e26f8391c4d`; no canonical change at screen.

Resolve `task-d66dd22cf7de` inspected the original
[1998 Land Administration Law reproduction](https://jjjcz.mee.gov.cn/djfg/gjflfg/fl/199808/t19980801_444213.html),
promulgation/effective-date paragraph and Articles31,33,44-45. Equal quantity
and quality replacement is required, but fees are allowed where reclamation
is infeasible or inadequate; aggregate conservation is a provincial duty,
with approved off-site exceptions. January1,1999 is the legal effective date,
not a verified common local compliance date. The
[original1998 implementing regulations](https://xzfg.moj.gov.cn/mobile/law/detail?LawID=1215),
Order256, Articles13,16,18-20, were also inspected: city/county government
responsibility and simultaneous conversion/replacement approvals do not
establish that every replacement occurred within the paper's city polygons.

The [Guangdong original2001 government gazette](https://www.gd.gov.cn/zwgk/gongbao/2001/34/content/post_3360608.html)
provides stronger local implementation evidence. Order66, promulgated
November13,2001 and effective January1,2002, Articles7-9 and13, explicitly
permits upper-level reserve use and provincially arranged paid transfers of
surplus replacement indicators between cities/counties, alongside a local
replacement default and off-site exceptions. This verifies one province's
rule, not nationwide realization. Cross-city substitution therefore cannot
be assumed absent before the2018 national platform; that extension is not
evidence of the first possible interjurisdictional replacement.

A [commercial reproduction of national2001 No374](https://www.kingland119.com/tools/policy/27011/detail)
was inspected in full, SectionsII,IV-V. It reports local replacement as the
default, provincial organization of off-site replacement when reserves are
insufficient, and project ledgers from2002. Its text is reported rather than
treated as an authenticated official original. The
[Hubei agency historical explanation](https://zrzyt.hubei.gov.cn/bmdt/ztzl/yggdbhjyjyyd/xsjlyggd/202011/t20201127_3057534.shtml)
corroborates the instrument identity and ledger history, not every historical
city's realized substitution. The original No374 remains unrecovered.

Admission is blocked pending current paper/replication evidence that closes
the actual locality-bound replacement exposure. Publisher full text and
SSRN current-manuscript retrieval failed, including a direct normal-UA403;
only the2021 draft methods are inspected. Statutory replacement is verified,
but treating border terrain as each city's binding replacement cost still
requires explaining fees, provincial reserves/transfers, local enforcement
and their timing. Inspect the final spatial construction, original No374
and relevant implementation evidence or diagnostics before admission. Do
not manufacture a Guangdong-only paper application, or publish the draft's
city-wide constraint as an unqualified national legal rule. No canonical
admitted; ready unchanged.

### Foreign-investment catalogue restriction changes — 2026-10-04

This targeted *Review of International Economics* extension belongs to the
specialist international-economics layer, below this campaign's general and

and [author publication list](https://yang-liang.github.io/)
identify Liang, Lovely and Zhang, *Targeted Liberalization: China's foreign
investment regulation reform and its post-WTO-accession export surge*,
RIE33(1),166-206 (2025; first-online2024). Screen `task-0fe72e207f15`.

The [author-uploaded typeset text](https://www.researchgate.net/publication/387927378_Targeted_Liberalization_China%27s_foreign_investment_regulation_reform_and_its_post-WTO-accession_export_surge)
was inspected through its full indexed transcription: Section2/Table1,
Sections3.2-3.3 and4 (printed early-view pp7-8,12-17). It uses separate
industry-year indicators for any encouraged and any restricted activity,
with neutral industries as the omitted category. It aggregates ASIE1998-2007
manufacturing firms by four-digit SCIC industry and ownership; the
SCIC1994-to2002 concordance matters. Twenty-four of31 industries losing
restrictions in2002 also became encouraged, so these are not interchangeable
treatments. The entry description uses differences in unique firm counts,
not automatically gross first-ever firm births. Domestic comparison in DDD
requires defending absence of indirect competitive effects.

Retain restriction removal for resolve: inspect original1997/2002 catalogue
clauses and effective dates, recover Sheng-Yang coding to SCIC and specific
activities, distinguish JV-only/equity-cap relaxation from encouragement
and actual subsidy receipt, and retain subsequent2004/2007 changes and tariff/
PNTR overlap. No primary catalogue or code inspected yet. Do not certify
exogeneity from insignificant leads, or count the whole reform twice because
the paper includes two policy variables. No canonical publication at screen.

Resolve `task-9e80668112b0` inspected the
[NDRC original2002 catalogue](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/fzggwl/200507/t20050707_960602_ext.html),
its promulgation paragraph, manufacturing lists and attachment. Order21 was
issued March11 and effective April1,2002, replacing the1997 edition.
Encouraged activities can still require joint ventures or Chinese control;
the attachment retains a50-percent foreign share cap for vehicle manufacture.
Restricted manufacturing activities also include entries without an explicit
ownership clause. Thus the paper's any-restricted industry indicator is not
an independently verified equity-cap variable. Services have their own
phased opening schedules and are outside this manufacturing application.

The complete five-page [State Council Order346 reproduction](https://www.caac.gov.cn/XXGK/XXGK/DWZC/201602/P020160225546572000697.pdf)
was inspected, particularly Articles4,8,10-12 and16-17. Unlisted projects are
permitted; ownership conditions are a separate catalogue provision. Chinese
control means at least51 percent in aggregate, whereas relative control
compares aggregate Chinese investment with each foreign investor. Export
orientation and central/western location can qualify for conditional relief;
restricted project approval cannot simply be treated as unrestricted local
approval. Hong Kong, Macao and Taiwan investors are covered by analogy;
this does not change the mainland location boundary for the collection.

The [SAT transition notice](https://shanghai.chinatax.gov.cn/zcfw/zcfgk/qysds/200606/t286942.html),
Guo Shui Fa2002 No63, dated May30, was inspected in full. Paragraphs1-2 tie
new encouragement classification to project or capital-increase approval
after April1 and preserve old tax preferences for qualifying earlier
approvals. This is a tax transition, not proof that all ownership restrictions
were grandfathered. The portal's2006 posting is not the reform date.

Admission remains blocked: neither the complete original1997 activity list
nor the Sheng-Yang480-industry coding and activity-to-SCIC crosswalk was
recovered from the inspected sources. Their absence leaves the actual
pre/post restriction-removal assignment unverified, not merely the outcome
dataset unavailable. Reopen with the original list and coding or an
auditable clause-level reconstruction, checking overlapping encouragement,
regional/export exceptions and subsequent revisions. Retain the published
application as a lead; do not publish an equity-cap case by relabeling its
category indicator. No canonical admitted and no ready increment.

Screen `task-ce8be4e1f10c` examines Fe, Liang and Lovely (2025),
*Follow thy neighbor: The role of first exporters*, JUE150,103813,

The publisher metadata and both author publication pages confirm identity;

introduction. Later retrieval of pp16-20 timed out; those pages are not
claimed read. This is the March2023 draft, not certified final methods.

The draft uses confidential2000-2006 customs transactions and registered
addresses in nine mainland cities. It identifies a district's first observed
HS2-product/destination exporter, excludes2000 first observations, and defines
nearby peers by shared subdistrict. Earlier unobserved exports, office rather
than factory addresses, mixed geocoding systems and endogenous spatial sorting
matter. Its stated design compares finer locations within broader district
controls; it does not report randomized export entry in inspected sections.
Do not turn firm proximity or the abstract's spillover estimate into an
intrinsically exogenous shock. This differs from capital-import spatial-ring IV.

Retain only for resolution of whether final methods provide a defensible
conditional exposure: inspect complete empirical design, exact paired risk
set/timing and inference, historical boundaries, address/data access and
selection diagnostics. If the final design supplies no credible assignment
contrast for the collection purpose, skip rather than publish. No canonical
change or ready increment at screen; no paper or customs microdata stored.

Resolve `task-0c0c38701e9b` recovered and inspected the same manuscript's
printed pp16-24 directly in memory, Sections4.2-5.1. The dyad sample pairs
a district-product-destination first exporter in t-1 with firms already
located in that district. Shared subdistrict is the exposure and the peer's
next-year route entry the outcome. Equation1 has district-year,
product-destination-year and peer effects; Equation2 adds district-product-
destination and peer-year effects. Reported inference clusters by district,
not by millions of dyads. Neither specification is a randomized assignment
or an inspected external instrument. Lagging exports does not eliminate
local joint shocks or sorting; the draft explicitly retains dyad confounding.

There is a material risk-set ambiguity: p17 requires no previous export of
product p to country c, while p19's variable description requires no previous
export to country c. Those select different firms. Historical subdistrict
boundaries/source vintage, tie handling among first exporters and coding
cannot be verified from the inspected material. Publisher and author sharing
links remain403; current author pages identify the final publication but
provide no inspected updated methods/code. Block admission until lawful final
methods or replication close this risk set and spatial assignment provenance.
The exposure is useful to revisit under an explicit conditional identification
argument, not rejected merely for being observational. No canonical admitted;
ready unchanged. Reopen with final/accepted text and historical boundary/code
documentation, rather than repeating title/abstract searches.

### Native-customs institutional takeover — 2026-10-04

Screen `task-ba3d0796d0f5` retains Gan Jin (2023), *Circle of fortune:
The long-term impact of Western customs institution in China*, JDE 163,
103075, [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jdeveco.2023.103075).
The [final typeset paper](https://cbdb.hsites.harvard.edu/sites/g/files/omnuum3101/files/cbdb/files/circle_of_fortune_the_long-term_impact_of_western_customs_institution_in_china.pdf)
was inspected through its publicly indexed full PDF, Sections 3-6.2,
Tables 1-2 and Appendix B (printed pp. 3-7 and 19-20).

The paper studies the 1902 transfer of Native Customs stations within fifty
li (approximately 25 km) of Maritime Customs stations. Actual county takeover
is the treatment; county-area coverage by 25-km circles instruments it.
This is neither a centroid-distance RD nor treaty-port opening. The sample
excludes treaty-port counties and Kiaochow, retaining 116 neighboring counties;
36 transferred stations map to 16 treated counties. Controls may lack a native
station or have an untransferred one. Historical counties join to 2000-2010
night lights. Section 4 cites CHGIS v6 while Table 1 cites v5; retain this
unresolved version discrepancy.

Resolve should recover the historical station roster and county crosswalk,
the fifty-li administrative rule and exceptions, and circle reconstruction.
Appendix B points to Inspector-General circulars, including a letter reproduced
on p. 462 of the 2003 collection, and 1907 customs returns; these originals
have not yet been inspected. Distinguish this mechanism from treaty-port
network candidate `candidate-cbdde43faed6`. Geographic exclusion, endogenous

reconciliation found no existing appointment-authority canonical or

### CER firm-birth tax-collection jurisdiction — 2026-10-03

Screen `task-360cf6139c2a` evaluates one source in the existing China
Economic Review expansion: Xing, Li and Xu, *The impact of tax enforcement
on corporate energy efficiency: Evidence from a tax collection reform in
China*, volume87, October2024,102260,
[DOI10.1016/j.chieco.2024.102260](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.chieco.2024.102260).
The publisher's indexed abstract/preview and the
[coauthor university profile](https://bs.csu.edu.cn/info/1780/23646.htm)
establish identity and a mainland industrial-firm application. Direct
ScienceDirect access returned403; no full CER methods, energy-efficiency
formula, appendix, bandwidth or replication code was inspected. Preview
claims, including20,448 observations in2003-2007, remain leads. This is
not a complete journal archive pass or a verified journal ranking.

The [SAT original Notice8](https://www.chinatax.gov.cn/chinatax/n810341/n810765/n812203/200201/c1209868/content.html)
was inspected in full, SectionsI-VII and signature. Issued16January2002,
it assigns enterprise-income-tax collection for ordinary newly registered
enterprises from1January2002 to state tax bureaus. Existing businesses
keep their original collection authority. Local-bureau exceptions cover
mergers of locally administered firms, splits of locally administered
firms and conversions of locally administered taxable public institutions;
mere registration changes do not reassign the bureau. Pre-cutoff business
registration followed by later tax registration retains the old rule.
Special sectors and personal/partnership income-tax rules have separate
jurisdiction. Legal scope is therefore not simply every firm's founding
year at or after2002. Bureau assignment is distinct from simultaneous
central-local revenue sharing, Golden Tax invoice digitization and the
2008 statutory-rate reform. Prior DOI/title/mechanism searches found no
canonical firm-birth bureau case; existing2002 income-tax-sharing
candidates concern regional fiscal exposure and cannot substitute for it.

An inspectable related application is Cai, Chen and Wang's
[NBER Working Paper25146, October2018](https://www.nber.org/papers/w25146),
[44-page PDF](https://www.nber.org/papers/w25146.pdf). PDFpp5-21 were read
in memory, specifically Sections2-5. This is a **working paper**, not a
verified journal publication and not the CER article's manuscript.
It uses birth month aroundJanuary2002 to instrument tax paid divided by
sales in a fuzzy RD/2SLS innovation application; government-bureau change
and effective-tax dose are not the same variable. Its cohort polynomials
have separate slopes and errors clustered by firm cohort; later tables
use2000-2003 birth cohorts. The authors report a lower state-bureau tax
burden in this sample, whereas the CER preview attributes more lenient
enforcement to local bureaus. Do not choose an enforcement sign for the
institution or treat the two samples as equivalent.

The working paper cleans ASIF manufacturing observations for2002-2007,
obtaining472,180 observations before narrower analyses. Its patent file is
described as records approved by1May2014, yet later passages discuss
applications and different approval counts; the applicant universe and
grant censoring require reconciliation. The merge uses firm name, address
and CEO name, not a demonstrated common patent-firm identifier. R&D data
exist only in2005-2007 and worker-education data only in2004. The main
patenting horizon includes2007-2010, which can overlap later tax changes;
avoid presenting every outcome as a contemporaneous2002-2007 panel.
Firm birth cohort, age, survival and above-scale inclusion are substantive
comparison risks. Density and re-registration plots in the paper do not
prove that all these risks disappear. The inspected results say R&D's
coefficient is not statistically significant, despite stronger summary
language; this is not evidence that all innovation inputs robustly rise.

Retain one `china-variation` candidate for the legal firm-registration
bureau assignment. Resolve must obtain a fully inspected published
application (the CER source or another clearly identified use), reconcile
its exceptions, treatment coding, data connection and actual diagnostics,
and inspect later jurisdiction amendments before extending cohorts.
NBER25146 supplies genuine related design evidence but must not fill
missing CER energy methods. Related publication leads, not inspected
full texts, include CER2021 bribery
`10.1016/j.chieco.2020.101578` and Management Science land-finance
`10.1287/mnsc.2021.4191`. No canonical file was created in screen.

### EER paired reconstruction aid and manufacturing — 2026-10-03

Screen `task-7aa13ea9c5b9` extends this campaign to one European Economic
Review source, not the journal's complete archive: Bulte, Xu and Zhang,
*Post-disaster aid and development of the manufacturing sector: Lessons
from a natural experiment in China*, volume101, January2018, pp441-458,
[DOI10.1016/j.euroecorev.2017.10.019](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.euroecorev.2017.10.019).
The inspected [author institution metadata](https://research.wur.nl/en/publications/post-disaster-aid-and-development-of-the-manufacturing-sector-les/)
supports publication identity. This is a bounded general-economics
extension motivated by mainland regional manufacturing and development,
not a claimed SSCI ranking or completed journal audit. DOI, title and
paired-aid mechanism searches found no existing canonical case.

The [NBER2013 meeting program](https://conference.nber.org/confer/2013/CEf13/program.html)
links the authors' [45-page working paper](https://conference.nber.org/confer/2013/CEf13/Zhang.pdf),
*Does Aid Promote or Hinder Industrial Development? Quake Lessons from
China*. Its pp7-24, Tables9-10 and adjoining table text were inspected in
memory. This establishes an actual empirical application, not merely a
policy proposed in an abstract. It does **not** establish that the final
2018 paper retained those specifications. Preserve this version boundary
until the final article and appendix are inspected.

The working paper studies centrally paired reconstruction assistance
after the12May2008 earthquake. Its four Sichuan county groups are18
paired recipients,15 damaged non-paired counties,9 undamaged neighboring
counties and96 other undamaged counties. The authors use2004-2011 county
GDP and sector shares, with2008-2011 coded post; census employment uses
2000/2010, with1990/2000 as a pre-period comparison. Assignment combines
disaster severity and donor economic capacity, rather than randomized
recipient selection. Undamaged neighbors can have migration, network and
construction-demand exposure, so they are not automatically clean
controls. Paper-reported aid calculated from donor fiscal data must be
distinguished from realized disbursements and from civil or central aid.
Historical county boundaries, census employment denominators and the
exact dose formula remain follow-up work. Hotel prices in Table10 start
inOctober2008, after the quake; they do not supply a pre-quake price panel.
These details belong to the inspected2013 version only.

The [MOF Budget Department's August2008 account](https://yss.mof.gov.cn/gongzuodongtai/200808/t20080801_60276.htm),
paragraph on reconstruction investment accounting, verifies a floor:
annual assistance in physical work is considered at **at least1% of the
donor's previous-year local fiscal revenue**. It does not verify actual
cash paid to each county. A [full secondary reproduction of Notice53](https://www.chinaacc.com/new/63/73/157/2008/6/wa08836155019168002560-0.htm),
SectionsI-III, reports a three-year horizon,18 Sichuan recipient counties,
additional Gansu/Shaanxi arrangements and matching that considers donor
capacity, damage and pre-existing emergency links. Its roster still needs
an inspected official original; the Zhejiang Gazette PDF lead returned405.
The [NDRC-hosted national reconstruction plan](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/qt/200809/t20080924_967737.html),
State Council Notice31 of2008, was recovered by direct HTTP after the web
reader timed out. ChapterI Section1, ChapterXIII Section6 and footnote7
were inspected: the plan additionally names four Gansu and two Shaanxi
aid recipients. Its
39-county Sichuan planning scope must not be substituted for the paper's
18 paired recipients or15 damaged comparison counties.

The [author's research page](https://sites.google.com/site/sherzodba/research)
and [publisher abstract](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0165176526002685)
identify Akhundjanov's2026 Economics Letters reassessment,
[DOI10.1016/j.econlet.2026.113074](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.econlet.2026.113074).
Only metadata and abstract were inspected. It challenges synthetic-control
inference and robustness in the2018 application. Do not import synthetic
control into the2013 working paper or conclude that every possible use of
paired assistance is invalid. Resolve the published design, primary
matching roster, data joins and the full critique before deciding whether
this case can enter canonical as grounded or contested. Screen retains
one `china-variation` candidate; no canonical record or ready increment.

Resolve `task-1efbec19e3f5` recovered the official Notice53 reproduction

official roster gap, superseding the screen-stage secondary-only limit.
The decree is dated11June2008; SectionI sets a three-year horizon and
expressly considers donor capacity, recipient damage and earlier
emergency-assistance relationships. These are selection determinants,
not evidence of random allocation. SectionII names these18 Sichuan pairs:
Shandong–Beichuan, Guangdong–Wenchuan, Zhejiang–Qingchuan,
Jiangsu–Mianzhu, Beijing–Shifang, Shanghai–Dujiangyan,
Hebei–Pingwu, Liaoning–Anxian, Henan–Jiangyou, Fujian–Pengzhou,
Shanxi–Maoxian, Hunan–Lixian, Jilin–Heishui, Anhui–Songpan,
Jiangxi–Xiaojin, Hubei–Hanyuan, Chongqing–Chongzhou and
Heilongjiang–Jiange. Guangdong, chiefly Shenzhen, also supports severely
affected Gansu areas, and Tianjin supports Shaanxi;19 donor jurisdictions
and20 listed relationships must not be miscounted as18 nationwide pairs.
SectionII(4) assigns other damaged jurisdictions to within-province
assistance, so the paper's non-paired group is not necessarily unaided.
SectionIII includes infrastructure, expertise and market-based business
investment, and sets a minimum physical-work value relative to previous-year
