paper with the different2025 China Economic Review misallocation paper
or the previously blocked JDE2024 minimum-capital paper
`10.1016/j.jdeveco.2024.103304` (`task-24ad5bd22c28`). No dedicated
canonical business-registration pilot was found in the current ledger.

The candidate belongs to `china-variation`: it concerns businesses located
in mainland Guangdong, with manufacturing as the main analysis and retail,
wholesale and services as supporting entry outcomes. The institution is a
package of reduced registration costs and changed post-entry oversight,
not a randomized removal of one capital constraint. Section2 describes
capital subscription in place of mandatory paid-in verification; fewer
pre-registration certificates; annual reporting in place of inspection;
relaxed address requirements; and consolidated online administration.
These are paper-reported components whose local start dates, exceptions
and legal differences must be resolved before canonical admission. The
paper's footnote9 gives an apparent problematic RMB300,000 minimum for
multi-shareholder LLCs. Verify the contemporaneous Company Law instead of
adopting that amount as an official fact.

Sections2.2/3 use21 cities, beginning with Dongguan and Foshan in2012,
followed by ten other cities in2013/early2014 and the remaining nine at
national expansion inMarch2014. Figure A1 labels eight rollout months:
September/December2012, March/October/November/December2013,
January/March2014. Its map colors do not yield a reliable city-date table
from extracted text; no complete mapping is claimed. Figure A2 identifies
the twelve early cities as Foshan, Dongguan, Zhuhai, Shenzhen, Yangjiang,
Zhanjiang, Zhongshan, Huizhou, Qingyuan, Guangzhou, Meizhou and Yunfu.
Recover each implementation month from readable official documents and
the paper's actual treatment coding; announcements can precede execution
by several months. Early pilot content and implementation also differed:
Foshan/Dongguan's entry response is delayed until the national expansion.
Their exclusion is therefore a substantive regime check, not proof that
the original bundle was identical everywhere.

Eq1/Table1 use log new-entrant counts at city-two-digit-manufacturing-
industry-month, January2009-December2016. Treatment starts in the local
implementation month. The preferred specification has city-industry,
city-year, city-calendar-month and industry-month-of-sample effects,
beginning-of-sample city-industry firm-count weights, and **city-industry**
clustered errors, not city-only clustering. Table1 has54,336 observations;
log entry-rate regressions have53,709 because cells with zero lagged
incumbents have undefined rates. The reported0.245 log coefficient is not
automatically an exact24.5% change. Eq2 groups tails outside the minus12
to plus15 monthly event window and references the month before adoption.
Identification relies on no differential unobserved within-city-year
monthly shocks after the controls, not a demonstrated random pilot.
City-industry clusters do not erase concern about21 city-level assignments.

All cities eventually adopt: the full2009-2016 TWFE result does not retain
a never-treated city group afterMarch2014. Appendix B1 uses not-yet-treated
comparisons and truncation for Sun-Abraham; imputation excludes2014 onward
and drops the two early cities in2013 to estimate untreated city-year
effects. Appendix B2/Table A9 excludes post-February2014 observations,
the four pre-adoption months, early pilots, or uses distant Jiangsu/
Shandong comparisons. These checks address anticipation, spillovers and
staggered-treatment contamination with different samples; they do not
authorize treating later national adoption as a fresh untreated contrast.

The Business Registry Database covers January2009-December2016; Firm
Annual Reports cover2008-2016. Registration provides incorporation dates,
city/industry/ownership and capital; the annual reports provide financial
outcomes. The record must distinguish monthly city-industry entry from
annual firm exit/size/productivity requirements. Registry/shareholder/
personnel linkage identifiers and stable legal versus operating locations
remain to recover, not assumed available through a universal ID.
Zhuhai is included in entry but excluded from exit/size/productivity
because annual reports are mostly missing post-reform. Annual-report
filing dips in2013 during the reporting-system transition. Employment
begins only in2013. The authors' universal-registration claim does not
make financial-report outcomes universal or cure differential reporting.

Exit is initially inferred from stopping annual reports; revocations were
few in2014 and nearly ceased in2015. Rectified2017/2018 revocations supply
a robustness definition. Eq3 measures exit in year t+1 and omits2016 for
right-censoring. Missing reporting is not literal legal dissolution.
Size/productivity Eq4 compares entrants and exiting firms with incumbents,
with city-year and industry-year controls; an entrant means its first full
calendar year **after** incorporation. Incumbents face competition too,
so this is a relative cohort effect, not an untreated-incumbent effect.
Appendix B1's two nonstaggered subsamples produce uneven size/productivity
results: one finds entrants larger and entrant-productivity changes small
and insignificant; do not repeat a claim of uniform robustness.

The ESIEC2018 survey provides retrospective industry-level registration-
cost changes. `RegCost` is an above-median reduction category inferred
from this later survey, not an independently assigned pre-policy dose.
Productivity uses a structural control-function approach without early
labor information, National Tax Survey wage/markup inputs and a tax-based
intermediate-input imputation. Appendix C explicitly discusses omitted
labor, tax measurement and selection. A researcher seeking only entry
need not obtain all these productivity inputs; a researcher seeking TFP
cannot replace them with incorporation counts or capital alone.

The publisher-linked replication lead is
[Dataverse DOI10.7910/DVN/XSVITM](https://dataverse.harvard.edu/dataset.xhtml?persistentId=doi:10.7910/DVN/XSVITM).
The API returned403 and the page a robot-verification screen; no README,
file inventory, code or accessible underlying microdata was inspected.
The paper's official Guangdong certificate-list link was also inaccessible
through the web tool. These are specific follow-up gaps, not evidence of
missing replication materials. Retain a candidate for a separate resolve
task covering legal package boundaries, exact city-month coding, joins,
reporting/exit measurement, final-version differences and duplicate identity.
No canonical record or ready increment is produced by this screen, and
no paper or restricted data file is stored.

Primary-source follow-up during the same screen inspected Shenzhen's
[original2013 gazette](https://www.sz.gov.cn/zfgb/2013/gb817/content/post_5002576.html),
not the later2020 consolidated version. It dates adoption to30October2012
and effectiveness to1March2013 (Article43). Articles9/12 retain some
sector approvals and distinguish incorporation from permission to operate.
Articles16-18 remove paid-in registration/verification for LLCs;
Articles29-33 replace inspections with reports and abnormal-operation
listing. Nonfiling need not mean dissolution. The separately inspected
[online-registration measure](https://www.sz.gov.cn/zfgb/2013/gb845/content/post_4969358.html)
starts1August2013 (Article19), with Article18 exclusions. Do not assign
every component the March date. The national2014 explanation remains a
search lead only. The inspected
[SAIC Order22 historical regulation](https://www.beijing.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengcefagui/qtwj/200512/t20051227_780127.html),
effective1January2006, Article10, sets the ordinary LLC minimum at
RMB30,000, not the manuscript's RMB300,000; one-person LLCs require
RMB100,000 and stock companies RMB5million, with sector exceptions.
Articles6/11 require verification and initial payment constraints.
Its historical text is useful despite the host's current-validity label;
it does not supersede later reforms. Shenzhen evidence cannot establish the
other twenty cities' schedules or prove rollout exogenous.

Resolve `task-bd156644df9f` inspected the author appendix's Figure A1
visually (PDF p67/A21), rendering in memory without storing the paper.
It is an unlabeled city-polygon map with eight month colors, not a
city-date table. Text extraction supplies the legend but not the mapping;
the map alone cannot establish a checked21-city exposure crosswalk.
Figure A2 names the early cities but does not replace that crosswalk.

Two additional official local texts were inspected. Zhongshan's
[中府〔2013〕113号](https://www.zs.gov.cn/zwgk/fggw/bsgfxwj/content/post_830434.html),
30October2013, Section3, schedules citywide implementation inNovember2013.
Section2 retains licensed activities, specified paid-in-capital sectors
and obligations to contribute; reporting noncompliance enters an abnormal
operation list rather than automatically proving closure. Yunfu's
[云府办函〔2014〕2号](https://www.yunfu.gov.cn/scjgj/xxgk/xzgg/content/post_347652.html),
6January2014 (web posting14January), gives component-specific deadlines
in January and March, sector/organizational exclusions, and qualified
residential-address use. A work-plan deadline is not evidence that all
components actually began simultaneously across the city.

The PKU-hosted102-page PDF found by search
`https://www.isss.pku.edu.cn/docs/2020-09/20200923150028629320.pdf`
is an unverified historical crosscheck lead. Its search result distinguishes
county/town pilots from later citywide rollout; in-memory extraction did
not recover the relevant table and it was not visually inspected. Do not
promote search-result dates into either official facts or the REStat
paper's coding. A Dongguan2012 gazette search lead
`https://www.dg.gov.cn/attachment/0/152/152990/3948472.pdf`
returned404 on direct retrieval. These are narrowly described access
limitations, not a claim that all relevant local evidence is unavailable.

The distinct candidate is retained but this resolve ends **blocked**,
without canonical admission. Identity as a bundled local deregulation is
clear; the missing link is the complete paper-used city-month assignment
and its treatment of earlier subcity pilots, phased components and annual
cohort coding. Registration/annual-report joins also lack an inspected
identifier/coding account. The public manuscript and three local regimes
are valuable evidence but do not close these links for the full sample.
The final publisher version and Dataverse file/code inventory were not
recovered in the screen; no absent-code claim is made. Reopen with an
inspectable treatment table/replication README and assignment code, or a
checked local implementation crosswalk reconciled with the manuscript.
Do not relax this specific gap into a generic data-access condition simply
to increase the ready count. Other collection can proceed while preserving
this candidate, source history and the legal corrections already obtained.

### Energy Economics product-level export rebate cut — 2026-10-03

Screen `task-de3e8592e34a` retains Lu, Zhang and Li, *The impact of export
VAT rebate reduction on firms' pollution emissions: Evidence from Chinese
enterprises*, Energy Economics120 (2023),106630, DOI
`10.1016/j.eneco.2023.106630`. The published20-page
[RUC-hosted paper](https://ae.ruc.edu.cn/docs/2023-05/baadc0cd801949d0ae821e1bfb12f04e.pdf)
was read in memory, including policy background, Sections3-6, Tables2-6,
and the printed appendix; no paper or restricted microdata was stored.
This is a bounded source in the existing Energy Economics firm/environment
lane, not a whole-journal audit. No DOI/title/mechanism match was found;
the product schedule is distinct from the existing2004 local fiscal
financing-share change in export VAT refunds.

The paper fixes firms' product portfolios in2006. `RebateCore` equals one
if any of their three largest products by export value is on the policy
adjustment list; `RebateFull` uses any export product; `RebateShr` weights
affected products by2006 export value, not their number. The policy starts
1July2007, but Eq4 defines `Post07` as2008 onward and Eq5 uses2007 as the
reference year. The figures' wording that2007 is before the policy does
not change the legal date:2007 is partly treated and cannot be called a
fully untreated baseline. Non-exporters and exporters without an affected
core product belong to the stated comparison; Section5.4.5 loosely calls
the controls non-exporters. Resolve should preserve this broader coding
and the paper's1:10 nearest-neighbor robustness, not silently restrict it.

The paper separately matches Customs to ASIF for export outcomes and AESPF
to ASIF for environmental outcomes, using names, institutional numbers,
addresses and contact information rather than a universal cross-source ID.
The environmental regression retains34,126 firms seen before and after,
1,725 treated and32,401 controls; Table3 uses180,481 observations.
ASIF-Customs and ASIF-AESPF pools contain75,465 and111,474 firms,
respectively, over2000-2013, not the same sample. City-clustered errors,
firm/year effects and table-listed city effects are the baseline; city-year
and industry-year effects are robustness variants. ASIF's post2011
threshold is printed as11million yuan on p6, an apparent source error to
check against official data documentation, not adopt as an official rule.
The appendix reports a balanced2005-2011 subsample and entry/exit checks;
neither proves away survival or matching selection. Table A3's industry
heterogeneity signs differ from the prose on p13, so do not inherit the
prose's uniform dirty-industry claim.

The [MOF contemporaneous explanation](https://www.mof.gov.cn/zhengwuxinxi/caizhengxinwen/200805/t20080519_26420.htm),
dated19June2007, identifies2,831 adjusted products:553 rebate removals,
2,268 reductions and10 shifts to exemption, with special grandfathered
contracts. The [Notice90 legal text and attachment-image inventory](https://policy.mofcom.gov.cn/claw/clawContent.shtml?id=13441)
specify the customs-declaration export date for implementation and three
distinct lists. The first scanned page of each of attachments1 and2 was
visually inspected: columns identify serial number, commodity code,
commodity name and the new percentage, not the rate reduction in percentage
points. Attachment1 first-page rows1-20 set rates to0; attachment2 first-page
rows1-19 set rates to5. These pages concern animal/vegetable products and
do not by themselves verify the paper's manufacturing exposure list. The
remaining schedule pages were not inspected or transcribed; no complete
machine-readable policy table is claimed.
[Notice97,10July2007, on the official tax site](https://shanghai.chinatax.gov.cn/zcfw/zcfgk/jckss/200707/t285763.html)
clarifies product codes, exclusions and contract definitions. The candidate
must therefore recover the corrected list and reconcile HS vintage/ranges,
not equate every listed exemption with a rebate reduction. The paper's
claim that pollution-targeted cuts persisted through2008-09 does not
establish persistence for the entire original package.

Resolve should ground the exact list used for the firm treatment, the
application's product-to-firm joins and the legal exceptions. The
comparison requires plausible differential trends despite deliberate
pollution/product targeting, global financial crisis exposure and concurrent
domestic regulation. The paper's US-share and tariff/exchange controls,
event studies, random-label placebos and alternative effects do not prove
exogeneity. Financial outcomes, emissions and imported abatement equipment
are actual applications; credit-access heterogeneity is not a separate
randomly assigned bank-credit shock. Admission remains for a distinct
post-screen task; this screen does not create a canonical record.

Resolve `task-f3ce2621c91a` admits the distinct product-policy exposure as
`china-2007-export-vat-rebate-product-cuts`, grounded for conditional research
matching. It separates the July2007 legal clock from the paper's2008 post
coding, Customs-ASIF export requirements from the additionally linked
AESPF-ASIF pollution requirements, and product-policy exposure from the
related2004 fiscal-financing mechanism. The official NBS27March2011
[coverage statement](https://www.stats.gov.cn/xw/tjxw/tzgg/202302/t20230227_1918602.html),
Section3, was inspected: the2011 threshold rose from5million to20million
yuan, correcting rather than propagating the paper's11million description.
Crossref title/authors/volume/article metadata match the university-hosted
published paper; April2023 is the issue month and15March is online availability.

Institutional identity and the firm's reported portfolio assignment are
recoverable without pretending to have numerical reproduction. The exact
author list/HS aggregation, complete transcribed legal schedule, exceptions
in microdata, later reversals, matching code and outcome transformations
remain explicit reproduction conditions. The record does not count every
alternative exposure or outcome as a separate variation, use a bad survey
threshold as an official rule, or convert event-study/placebo success into
an exogeneity guarantee. No original record, paper or data asset was removed.

### World Development bank-entry reform screen — 2026-10-03

Screen `task-254fe1c2b377` evaluates Chen, Chen, Lou, Song and Wu,
*Bank deregulation and corporate environmental performance*, World
Development161 (2023),106106, DOI `10.1016/j.worlddev.2022.106106`.
This single-paper pass extends the development/firm-finance lane within
the existing World Development coverage; it is not an archive sweep.
The publisher article preview and coauthor
[Tao Chen's university page](https://personal.ntu.edu.sg/jtchen/)
confirm publication. The latter uses the alternate title *Bank Competition
and Corporate Environmental Performance* for the same volume/article.
DOI, title and mechanism searches found no existing canonical or candidate.

The preview indicates actual mainland-firm exposure to the2009 domestic
branch-entry reform, with city pre-reform state-bank branch concentration
and COD emission intensity. These are discovery leads, not verified
treatment coding. The treatment must be distinguished from foreign-bank
RMB lending opening and from realized bank entry, which can be endogenous.
The separate Gao/Ru/Townsend/Yang author-hosted2023 working paper concerns
the same reform but is not a published-version substitute for this source.

The [author-linked SSRN manuscript](https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=3633696)
lists51pages and a15May2023 revision; its page returned HTTP403 to the web
reader and remained at automatic browser security verification. No
verification challenge was bypassed. OpenAlex metadata identified an
[NTU institutional deposit](https://hdl.handle.net/10356/227321), but its
redirect returned HTTP405 to the network reader; no full text was inspected.
The screen therefore ends blocked, without canonical publication.

When legitimate full text becomes accessible, recover the exact baseline
city exposure denominator and vintage, sample years and firm-city joins,
policy eligibility for joint-equity versus city-commercial banks, and the
distinction between COD intensity and total discharge. Compare differential
stimulus, preexisting city credit trends and concurrent environmental
enforcement against the reported contrast. A policy name and paper prestige
do not establish these missing assignment and measurement details.

### JIE Chinese exporters and first US patent awards — 2026-10-03

Screen `task-ba5ce5361cad` retains `candidate-409fcecdc7f1` from Gong,
Li, Manova and Sun, *Tickets to the global market: First US patent award
and Chinese firm exports*, JIE157 (2025), DOI
`10.1016/j.jinteco.2025.104124`. This is a bounded single-paper pass in the
existing JIE trade/firm lane, not a journal-archive completion claim. The
[published26-page author PDF](https://yaoli.people.ust.hk/patent_ticket2025JIE.pdf)
was inspected at pp5-12, including institutional background, data,
Eq1-2, Section3.2 and Tables2-3. An older55-page2022 preliminary version
linked by another coauthor is not the current empirical authority.

The foreign institution actually assigns exposure to mainland Chinese
first-time applicants, so this is `global-china-variation`, not method-only.
Patent granting is endogenous; the excluded instrument is the assigned
examiner's prior approval rate, demeaned by art unit and first-action year.
The application uses **ultimate** first-application approval as treatment
but anchors export growth to the **first-action year**, not filing or final
grant. Eq2 defines symmetric growth between endpoint export values;
footnote16 describes positive exports at both endpoints. However, the
Table4 annualization and the different continuing-exporter description
on p15 require code-level resolution before that encoding is authoritative.
Art-unit clustered errors and
industry-by-application-year effects are reported.2,831 matched exporters
are the full matched population, not the baseline regression sample:
Table2 has1,156 continuing CCTS exporters and940 in the ASIE subset.

Section2 states2001-2016 patent outcomes and2000-2016 customs coverage;
ASIE2000-2013 is an extension, not mandatory for the customs-only contrast.
Pure assembly is excluded from the baseline, while ordinary and
processing-with-imports trade are retained. English-to-Chinese assignee
translation, company registration histories, address/industry validation
and customs identifiers underpin matching. Public patent data omit some
early abandoned applications, and unsuccessful pre2001 filings make
first-application status left-censored. Resolve needs examiner histories
that genuinely predate each target decision and the exact final code's
outcome, date, trimming and endpoint rules before reproduction.

The [historical USPTO MPEP E8R9 Chapter900](https://www.uspto.gov/web/offices/pac/mpep/old/e8r9/mpep-0900.pdf),
PDFp35, printed900-35, Section903.08(a-b), was inspected: initial
technology-center routing and supervisory classification/assignment are
administrative processes, **not a statutory uniform lottery**. The
[current Section909](https://www.uspto.gov/web/offices/pac/mpep/s909.html)
instead describes CPC-based automated routing with examiner portfolios,
docket and backlog factors; it must not be backdated to the paper's sample.
The paper acknowledges specialization and examiner-shopping concerns,
reports balance and alternative-demeaning tests, and does not directly
rule out specialization. Keep those limits when interpreting its
quasi-randomness claim; an IV first stage alone does not establish exclusion.

The [USPTO PatEx documentation page](https://www.uspto.gov/ip-policy/economic-research/research-datasets/patent-examination-research-dataset-public-pair)
was inspected for public prosecution histories and release differences.
The final paper links [Mendeley replication](https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/wbvtd6ykfg/1),
but the landing page and public metadata API returned access failures in
this pass; no code, licensed customs microdata or numerical replication is
claimed inspected. This candidate is deliberately separate from domestic
innovation subsidies and IPO approval records. Resolve must preserve
selection into patenting/export survival, examiner channels other than
approval and the difference between an IV-local effect and a general ATE.

Resolve `task-f17314e5a4ce` inspected the final paper through its baseline
results, robustness discussion and data-availability statement. Table4
labels the dependent variable annualized three-year export growth, whereas
Eq2 presents the symmetric endpoint change without an explicit annualizing
factor. More importantly, footnote16 on p8 requires exports at both
endpoints, while p15 describes exporting in the first-action year and at
least once in the subsequent three years. Those descriptions do not
identify the same conditioning sample. This matters because selection on
post-treatment exporting is part of interpreting the estimated effect,
not merely a formatting or replication convenience.

During resolve, the replication landing page became accessible in the
browser: version1, published27May2025, DOI `10.17632/wbvtd6ykfg.1`, CC BY4.0,
with `code`, `data`, and `ReadMe_GLMS.pdf`. The sample-creation folder lists
`1_create_CHN_application_sample.do`, `2_create_first_stage_sample.do`, and
`3_create_main_sample.do`. Its file preview does not support Stata source;
the public download returned HTTP403 outside the browser and the browser
download did not complete. File names and license were inspected, but
code contents and data were not. This resolves landing-page availability,
not the outstanding measurement issue. The candidate remains blocked
rather than entering canonical. Reopen when these sample-creation scripts
or an author clarification establish the annualization, export endpoint
and survival rules, as well as the examiner-history timing used in code.
No new ready record or numerical replication is claimed.

This is the expansion plan for the collection campaign after the field-top five journals. It is a coverage design, not a claim that SSCI or any single business-school list supplies a universally valid ranking. Journal quality and field relevance are related but not identical: a strong urban journal may be more useful for this project than a higher-ranked general journal that contains no recoverable spatial assignment. The queue therefore expands by **research value and auditability**, while keeping the same evidence gate at every tier.

The purpose is to find papers that actually use a Chinese policy, institutional boundary, historical event, infrastructure exposure, or other spatial assignment in a regional/urban question. A China case study without a usable exposure is not a variation. A paper that merely says a method could be applied in China is a transferable method, not a China variation. Search results, titles, abstracts, and a journal's reputation help discovery; they do not establish the assignment.

Publication recency is a soft ordering signal. When two leads have comparable China relevance, spatial substance, evidence access, and likely variation value, prefer the newer paper and keep its publication year visible in the source/task record. Recency never rescues a paper whose assignment is only implied, and it never removes an older paper that supplies a distinct or historically important variation. The collection is a knowledge base, not a news feed: a 2008 policy can be more useful than a 2026 descriptive article.

## Tiers and order of work

The five field-top lanes remain the first completion boundary: JUE, RSUE, JRS, JoEG, and EG. Expansion does not silently declare those lanes complete. Once a bounded pass and its repeat search are recorded for a field-top lane, work can rotate into the next tier while unresolved high-value candidates continue to take priority.

| Tier | Journal lane | Why it is useful here | Primary archive | Independent recall lane | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Field-top | *Journal of Urban Economics* | Core urban economics: land, housing, migration, transport, local public finance, pollution, and spatial labor markets. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/journal-of-urban-economics) | Crossref ISSN inventory plus IDEAS/RePEc and subject/citation search. | In progress in `sources/fieldtop-china-regional-urban-coverage.md` |
| Field-top | *Regional Science and Urban Economics* | Core regional/urban economics and spatial policy evaluation. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/regional-science-and-urban-economics) | Crossref ISSN inventory plus IDEAS/RePEc and subject/citation search. | In progress in field-top map |
| Field-top | *Journal of Regional Science* | Regional shocks, networks, commuting, resilience, and spatial inequality. | [Wiley](https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/journal/14679787) | Crossref ISSN inventory plus IDEAS/RePEc and citation/subject search. | In progress in field-top map |
| Field-top | *Journal of Economic Geography* | Spatial organization, agglomeration, location rules, and regional development. | [Oxford Academic](https://academic.oup.com/joeg) | Crossref ISSN inventory plus IDEAS/RePEc, JEL/subject search, and citation snowball. | In progress in field-top map |
| Field-top | *Economic Geography* | Place-based institutions and regional processes where exposure or comparison is recoverable. | [Taylor & Francis](https://www.tandfonline.com/journals/recg20) | Crossref ISSN inventory plus IDEAS/RePEc, JEL/subject search, and citation snowball. | In progress in field-top map |
| Specialist-1 | *Regional Studies* | Broad, high-quality urban and regional research spanning economic, social, political, and environmental dimensions of subnational change. | [Taylor & Francis](https://www.tandfonline.com/journals/cres20) | Crossref ISSN plus publisher archive, IDEAS/RePEc, and regional-science citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Specialist-1 | *Urban Studies* | International urban scholarship on cities and regions; useful for housing, migration, governance, transport, and urban policy. | [SAGE](https://journals.sagepub.com/home/usj) | Crossref ISSN plus SAGE archive and urban/regional citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Specialist-1 | *Environment and Planning A: Economy and Space* | Spatial political economy, regional development, land, infrastructure, and place-based institutions. | [SAGE](https://journals.sagepub.com/home/epn) | Crossref ISSN plus SAGE archive and geography/economic-geography search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Specialist-1 | *Papers in Regional Science* | Official Regional Science Association outlet; theory and methods applied to urban and regional phenomena. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/papers-in-regional-science) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and regional-science citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Specialist-1 | *Spatial Economic Analysis* | Spatial and geographic economics, regional policy, and spatial data methods. | [Taylor & Francis](https://www.tandfonline.com/journals/rsea20) | Crossref ISSN plus publisher archive and regional-science citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Economics-2 | *China Economic Review* (CER) | China-focused quantitative and analytical work on institutional change, policy, and performance; regional/urban relevance must still be demonstrated paper by paper. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/china-economic-review) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and China-policy/urban search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Economics-2 | *Journal of Comparative Economics* | Institutional and policy changes in China with credible regional or local assignment. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/journal-of-comparative-economics) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and institutional-comparison search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Economics-2 | *Journal of Development Economics* | Development policies, infrastructure, migration, and local institutions when the design has a real spatial margin. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/journal-of-development-economics) | Crossref ISSN plus Crossref/IDEAS and development-policy citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Economics-2 | *World Development* | Development interventions and regional shocks; keep only papers with a decision-useful spatial assignment. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/world-development) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and development/China search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Economics-2 | *Journal of Housing Economics* | Housing, land, and urban policy experiments or boundary designs in China. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/journal-of-housing-economics) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and housing-policy citation search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |
| Adjacent-economics | *Marketing Science* | Selective quantitative market and platform research only when the paper actually studies a mainland-China regional, development or firm exposure; not a substitute for the economics field-top lanes. | [INFORMS journal archive](https://pubsonline.informs.org/journal/mksc) | Institutional repositories plus IDEAS/RePEc and exact-title citation recall. | One targeted 2026 regional-development article screened on 2026-10-05; no journal-wide coverage claim or ranking assertion |
| Economics-2 | *Journal of Environmental Economics and Management* | Environmental regulation and spatial exposure when the regional/urban mechanism is central rather than incidental. | [ScienceDirect](https://www.sciencedirect.com/journal/jeem) | Crossref ISSN plus IDEAS/RePEc and environmental-policy/spatial search. | Planned; first bounded sweep not yet recorded |

This order is deliberately a queue, not a promise to exhaust every journal indexed by SSCI. SSCI membership, impact metrics, and business-school lists such as UTD can change and may not measure the field value relevant to a variation library. When a journal is added, record the official archive, ISSN, index or scope evidence available on the search date, the publication-year boundary, and the independent recall source. Do not write an unverified ranking into a task or canonical record.

## Broader China empirical collection — 2026-09-29

The current owner goal now includes non-spatial digital, labour, firm,
institutional, environmental and innovation questions; see the dated focus in
the live campaign map. The earlier lane descriptions explain the regional/urban
search history, not a veto on a paper used for the expanded goal. Journal
reputation still orders discovery rather than certifying an assignment.

A targeted *American Economic Journal: Economic Policy* source dated
2026-10-03 is Chen, Hu and Knittel, “Subsidizing Fuel-Efficient Cars: Evidence
from China's Automobile Industry” (2021, DOI `10.1257/pol.20170098`). This
single-paper pass does not claim an AEJ archive sweep. The
[AEA listing](https://www.aeaweb.org/articles?id=10.1257/pol.20170098)
establishes the published identity; the [MIT CEEPR working paper](https://ceepr.mit.edu/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/2018-003.pdf)
and [AEA online appendix](https://www.aeaweb.org/content/file?id=15567)
expose the study's model-level eligibility and catalog-wave design. The
[2010 joint-ministry rule](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/fggz/hjyzy/jnhnx/201006/t20100603_1134364.html)
(财建〔2010〕219号) supplies a primary institutional anchor. Screen task
`task-f59be3bf7daa` triages this source separately from EV subsidies,
old-car replacement, and municipal car-use restrictions. These sources do
not by themselves establish that the administrative order of catalog waves
was exogenous to model sales.

A targeted *Journal of Public Economics* source dated 2026-10-03 is Chen,
Li, Mao and Zhou, “Consumption stimulus with digital coupons: Heterogeneity
and policy design” (2026, DOI `10.1016/j.jpubeco.2026.105709`). This is a
single-paper extension, not an audit of the JPubE archive. The
[publisher listing](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0047272726001453)
establishes publication identity; the [full author manuscript](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2507.01365)
supplies the inspectable design, and the [Beijing policy interpretation](https://www.beijing.gov.cn/zhengce/zcjd/202207/t20220708_2767092.html)
confirms the municipal restaurant-coupon program but not platform-level claim
timing. Screen task `task-7fa0acdbab5c` retained the paper-used
quota-exhaustion contrast for resolution. Random sampling of users
and a counterfactual random-allocation exercise are not randomized coupon receipt.

Resolve task `task-c0817323e5d0` found a material date discrepancy, not a
second variation. The [original municipal measure](https://www.beijing.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengcefagui/202207/t20220707_2765551.html)
(京发改〔2022〕907号, item 11) authorizes restaurant coupons from July, and the

(京商生活字〔2022〕41号) names Ele.me as a first-round platform. A

states Ele.me's announced window was July 18–August 16, 2022; the inspected
author manuscript, Section 2.1 and equation (1), instead analyzes July
18–August 27. No platform or municipal extension establishing the extra eleven
days was found in this bounded source search. The manuscript also says failed
claim timestamps were unavailable (Section 3.1, footnote 12); its matched
ever-recipient-versus-never-recipient DID is not a narrow cutoff experiment.
Before canonical admission, recover the campaign extension or final paper and
platform rules that reconcile the 41-day treatment period; preserve the timing
conflict and selection limit rather than silently treating either date as settled.

The 2026-10-02 *Journal of International Economics* extension is a targeted
firm/trade-finance source, not a full journal inventory or a claim about its
ranking. Its [publisher article](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0022199626000826)
(DOI `10.1016/j.jinteco.2026.104292`) and independent

or random allocation of approvals is inferred from this single article.

One bounded JDE follow-up screened DOI `10.1016/j.jdeveco.2026.103918`, *Payroll
taxes, firm dynamism, and aggregate productivity* (task `task-6700556d01f6`).
The DOI was absent from canonical and candidate/task ledgers before this screen.
The official ScienceDirect article endpoint and Crossref DOI metadata were
checked, with the authors' Fudan publication page and Yu Liu's research page as

complete JDE archive sweep. Crossref and the authors identify volume 185,
February 2027; the Fudan posting is dated 2026-09-07. These are different dates,
and an exact online-publication date has not been verified.

The inspected Fudan abstract reports a transfer of social-insurance collection
from insurance agencies to tax authorities and a firm-entry/labour application.
That is sufficient to retain a source for resolution, not to publish its

attachment returned a human verification page rather than a PDF; the public
Elsevier retrieval endpoint returned core metadata without full text. Recovering
the paper/appendix, province-level reform chronology, sample rules and treatment
comparison is the next evidence step. Do not substitute indexed introductory
prose for inspected full text or conflate enforcement with a statutory-rate change.

### JDE government-VC source reopened — 2026-09-29

Task `task-da9612d2c333` revisits DOI `10.1016/j.jdeveco.2025.103599`,
*Industrial policy in a new era: Government venture capital in the US–China
trade war* (JDE 178, 2026). The July title-only skip in
`state/jde_china_batch_triage.md` remains historical; it is superseded for
screening by inspected manuscript evidence, not deleted. Gong's
[author publication page](https://rkgong.weebly.com/research.html) explicitly
connects the earlier title to this publication. No existing canonical or
candidate represents its exposure.

The inspectable [ABFER manuscript](https://www.abfer.org/media/abfer-events-2022/annual-conference/papers-coprpfin/AC22P2013_Coming-to-the-Rescue_the-Role-of-Government-Venture-Capital-in-the-US-China-Trade-War.pdf)
is dated January 5, 2022, not the published version. Pages 6–13 were inspected
in memory. Its assignment is U.S. tariff exposure of Chinese manufacturing
industries, not receipt of government VC. Section 2.3 uses 2017 product exports
to weight tariff burdens and divides industries at median average exposure.
Section 2.4 compares high/low exposure before/after 2018Q3 and examines
government/private-fund heterogeneity. Sections 2.2–2.4 document company/fund
registration links, investments and patents, a pre-existing-pair sample, and a
separate fund-industry-quarter panel. These are manuscript-reported constructions,
not independently verified data availability or final-publication coding.

There is a concrete reason not to publish yet. Manuscript page 10 places a
7.5% rate at September 1, 2019. The inspected official
[USTR notice, 85 FR 3741](https://ustr.gov/sites/default/files/enforcement/301Investigations/Notice_of_Modification-January_2020.pdf),
sections A–B and Annex, instead dates the reduction from 15% to 7.5% to
February 14, 2020. This establishes a discrepancy in the old text, not proof
that its underlying data or the final article is wrong. Resolve against the
published paper, appendix and tariff inputs before reconstructing exposure.
The [SSRN version page](https://ssrn.com/abstract=4218297) identifies an August
2025 revision, but its PDF retrieval returned HTTP 403 in this session.

The next task should keep the tariff instrument separate from endogenous
government funding and pre-existing local VC presence. It must recover the
HS-to-industry concordance, statutory rates versus exclusions, final sample
boundaries and diagnostics. A non-significant pre-trend test does not establish
that targeted industries would otherwise have evolved identically. This source
is retained as `global-china-variation`, not an admitted government-VC policy
experiment, and does not certify JDE coverage completion.

Resolve task `task-5925735e1df4` checked the final-version routes on the same
date. ScienceDirect again returned 403; the public Elsevier retrieval response
identified January 2026 and `openaccess=0`, with core metadata only; SSRN's
revision PDF returned 403. The HKUST research-summary endpoint failed with
502 and would not establish final coding even if accessible. No final appendix
or tariff input was inspected. The candidate is blocked pending a legitimately
accessible final/revised manuscript and its tariff construction; the old draft
remains a recoverable source lead, not a canonical case or a finding of error
in the published article. Repeated access attempts without a new route are not
the next collection priority.

This is a bounded 2024 source pass, not journal-wide coverage or a new economics
journal ranking. Independent [IDEAS metadata](https://ideas.repec.org/a/nat/nature/v630y2024i8018d10.1038_s41586-024-07500-2.html)
confirms publication identity. Published Methods and the
[AEA registry](https://www.socialscienceregistry.org/trials/8075) were inspected.
Registry assignment/sample descriptions conflict with the article and require

The linked Harvard Dataverse dataset `doi:10.7910/DVN/6X4ZZL` was not inspected:
its public dataset API returned HTTP 403 on this pass. Resolve should try an
accessible repository landing page or author-hosted code and registry history;
the paper's data-availability statement is not evidence of executed replication.

Resolve `task-1ccde5eae671` inspected the original registry version and 2025
change log, plus page 2 of the official publisher reporting summary. The latter
documents the final published allocation description and agrees with the article;
the registry history preserves an unresolved conflicting description rather
than demonstrating a corrected protocol. The new grounded record
`china-trip-hybrid-work-eligibility-experiment` follows the final published
application, with explicit reproduction/registration conditions and no claim of
independently audited personnel allocation. It distinguishes assignment from
uptake, the active experiment from later common access, and original participants
from later survivors. The older call-centre record remains untouched. The
repository landing route returned an empty HTTP 202 and the API remained 403;
deposit contents were not inspected.

Activation is a small, auditable change: add one lane with its official archive and independent index, record the date and endpoint, define broad recall terms (China, Chinese cities/provinces/prefectures/counties, land, housing, migration, transport, infrastructure, local public finance, environment, agglomeration, and historical or external shocks), and create only bounded screen tasks for retained leads. The first sweep should not be mixed with another journal, because otherwise an apparent lack of candidates cannot be attributed to a searchable boundary.

For each retained lead, the screen must answer only whether the paper actually used a Chinese or China-facing spatial exposure and whether a next evidence step is worth paying for. Resolve or ground must then close the identity, institutional boundary, assignment rule, timing, treatment/comparison, data connection, estimand, evidence limits, and principal failure conditions. If the full text or primary institutional source is inaccessible, keep the lead blocked or pending rather than replacing it with an abstract-based claim. Existing canonical records are reconciliation targets; they are never a reason to skip the paper's own evidence.

## Regional Studies activation snapshot — 2026-09-28

The first bounded *Regional Studies* activation uses the [official Taylor & Francis journal archive](https://www.tandfonline.com/journals/cres20) and a separate Crossref inventory for online ISSN `1360-0591`. The Crossref query covered articles dated from 2024-01-01 through 2026-12-31 and was retrieved on 2026-09-28. Title recall used China, Chinese, Hong Kong, Beijing, Shanghai, Shenzhen, Guangzhou, Tibet, and Xinjiang; a title match is only a lead, not evidence of an assignment. DOI/title reconciliation against `variations/`, `state/tasks.jsonl`, and `state/candidates.jsonl` found no existing record for `10.1080/00343404.2024.2438319`. The scan surfaced many descriptive innovation, governance, resilience, and spatial-structure papers. The bounded screen below was retained because its title identifies a specific China-wide place-based program with plausible regional exposure rather than simply a Chinese case.

| Online year | DOI | Source lead | Initial lane | Screen task |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2025 | `10.1080/00343404.2024.2438319` | *Unravelling regional inequality: the heterogeneous impact of China’s Great Western Development Program* | `china-variation` | `task-e270fd264daa` |

This lead is deliberately separate from the blocked JRS Western Development Strategy boundary-DID candidate (`10.1111/jors.70036`). A later screen must establish whether it uses the same 2000 program with a distinct exposure, outcome, and comparison or merely restates the same policy family. It must not create a canonical record from metadata alone.

## Relationship to the data repository

`Econ Data Know-How` remains complementary. This repository records what changed, who was exposed, when and where, how a researcher can encode treatment and comparison, and what can invalidate the design. The data repository records the datasets papers actually use, acquisition or reconstruction paths, coverage, identifiers, joins, access conditions, reproducibility, and data limitations. Link by DOI when useful, but do not copy data assets into this library or turn a data search into a second variation directory.

## Completion and reopening

The overall campaign is complete only relative to a dated, auditable set of active journal lanes. Every active lane needs a primary archive sweep, an independent recall sweep, DOI/title reconciliation, durable dispositions for all high-value leads, and a repeat search with no new unreviewed high-value source. Adding a new journal, archive endpoint, or materially better independent index reopens only that lane; it does not lower the admission threshold for the others. A tier can therefore grow without turning “more journals” into pressure to publish half-understood records.

### Top-journal one-source screen — 2026-09-30

This is one recent *Review of Economic Studies* source, not an exhaustive ReStud
archive pass. DOI `10.1093/restud/rdaf066`, *Women in the Courtroom: Technology
and Justice*, appears in volume 93, issue 3 (May 2026), with the publisher giving
an online publication date of 1 August 2025. The screen is `task-8e43cf71a11c`
and candidate `candidate-e09a7cc12698`.

The published article and the authors' 37-page manuscript use the 2016 China
Court Trial Online live-broadcast rollout; this is distinct from the 2013 China
Judgments Online platform that supplies judicial documents. The manuscript
reports 6.42 million individual-party civil judgments from 2014–2018, court-area-
quarter broadcast intensity (broadcast cases / total cases), court-area and
quarter fixed effects, and a Bartik instrument using 2014 within-court area shares
and contemporaneous prefecture-area broadcast intensity excluding the focal
court. It also reports case-level broadcast selection by lower courts, so that
individual broadcast status must not be treated as random.

Supreme People's Court pages verify the China Court Trial Online launch and
nationwide connection process. They give dates/counts that do not exactly match
the author's reported 383 courts by September 2016, another 762 by January 2017,
and 3,517 by December 2017. Resolve the chronology/count boundary, paper-version
identity, implementation instructions, construction of the rollout and intensity
variables, judgment-to-broadcast joins, and the IV assumptions before admission.
The current disposition is a candidate only; neither the title nor the abstract
is the evidentiary basis for canonical publication.

### JDE green-credit one-source screen — 2026-10-02

Task `task-310d56ef744c` screens Fan, Peng, Wang and Xu's *Greening through
finance?*, JDE 152 (2021), DOI `10.1016/j.jdeveco.2021.102683`, within the
expanded firm/environment/finance scope. This is one source, not a complete
journal sweep. No exact DOI or mechanism was found in canonical or task/candidate
records. The university-hosted published PDF was inspected in memory through
[the IMI HTTP route](http://www.imi.ruc.edu.cn/docs/2021-09/36be06f293e44c1894f5b7b5985f6e7b.pdf);
HTTPS certificate/connection failures and the publisher's 403 did not imply that
the paper itself was unavailable. PDF pages 4 and 8-10 cover the institution,
assignment, data and baseline specification, not just the abstract.

Candidate `candidate-b14409ff7659` preserves the actually used exposure:
environmental punishment history interacted with a February 2012 policy break,
with loan-level and annual firm-outcome applications kept distinct. It is not a
generic high-pollution-industry treatment. Resolve must clarify the paper's
punishment-cohort exclusions, official issuance/publication dates, subsequent
enforcement complements, loan selection and name-based data joins. The
[Shanghai finance authority's CBRC text](https://jrj.sh.gov.cn/zwdt-tz/20120223/0031-78222.html)
was inspected, including Articles 11, 17, 28 and 29; its page date and signature
date differ. The linked State Council Gazette endpoint returned 403 and remains
an uninspected date-resolution route. No canonical record is admitted at screen.

### JDE green-credit resolution — 2026-10-02

Task `task-057c3712389c` follows candidate `candidate-b14409ff7659`.
The Gazette route was recovered through direct HTTP retrieval, without storing
the document. The [State Council Gazette, 2012 issue 17](http://www.gov.cn/gongbao/content/2012/content_2163593.htm)
was inspected from the notice identifier through Article 30. It identifies
银监发〔2012〕4号 and a **29 January 2012** signature, unlike the Shanghai
reprint's 24 February signature and 23 February page metadata. Article 29 makes
effectiveness depend on publication, not the signature. The paper's February
2012 monthly post clock is a reported empirical choice; neither the signature
nor the inconsistent reprint establishes its exact publication or operational
start date. A dated CBRC announcement or equivalent primary publication record
is still needed to reconcile these clocks. The Gazette establishes customer
environmental/social-risk assessment and differential pricing (Article 11),
refusal of credit to noncompliant customers (17), and supervisory assessment
consequences (28), not a legally fixed treatment list equal to IPE penalties.

The university-hosted published article was further inspected at article pages
10-15 (PDF pages 11-16), Sections 5.2-6.2 and Tables 2-11. Together with the
previously inspected Sections 2, 3.4 and 4, this recovers the contrast and its
limits. Baseline `Punish_ft` switches on in the punishment year and remains on;
the national post indicator starts in February 2012. Footnote 23 excludes firms
punished after 2012, but does not unambiguously distinguish first punishment
from any subsequent punishment, or specify treatment of punishment within
2012 relative to February. This is a core cohort/sample-construction gap, not
permission to rewrite the baseline as a fixed pre-2012 treated group. Section
5.2.2 separately reports a fixed pre-guideline punishment-group robustness
exercise, with results in online Table A4; that table and its construction
materials were not inspected. Industry classifications and shift-share/peer
punishment IVs are additional robustness constructions, not new canonical
variations or established valid instruments.

Loan data cover 2009-2015 at one anonymous Big Five bank; firm-name joins link
them to ASIF and IPE penalties. The preferred Table 1 specification has 120,821
loan observations, not firms. `Floating` is 100 times the actual-minus-benchmark
rate divided by the benchmark; a coefficient in this variable is not the same
number of percentage points in the actual loan rate. Firm, subbranch-by-month
and maturity effects address different heterogeneity dimensions. Section 5.4
and Table 8 examine firm-year loan access, but the inspected text does not
fully recover the nonborrower risk set; an absence from this bank must not be
interpreted as absence of credit from every bank. ASIF financial outcomes and
AESPF emissions/intensity outcomes are distinct panels with outcome-specific
observation counts, not one common loan sample. ASIF's survey-threshold change
and name-based matching require explicit selection/linkage checks; detailed
annual sample windows and cleaning remain to be recovered from supplementary
materials rather than inferred from the loan window.

The main article reports event-study, matching, alternative clustering and
balanced-panel checks. Those are attributed claims where the relevant online
table/figure is unavailable. They do not prove random punishment, eliminate
unobserved enforcement shocks, or make firms without observed penalties truly
compliant. Concurrent environmental regulation and selective loan approval can
change the comparison independently of green-credit pricing. Emission levels
and intensity also differ: Section 6 reports scale reduction among small firms
and different abatement responses by size, not a universal technology upgrade.

Disposition: **blocked in the candidate layer**, with no canonical admission or
ready increment. The publisher supplement endpoint returned 403; the inspected
author research page links the paper but not a green-credit appendix. Retry when
a reviewable appendix/code or dated primary publication record is available.
Resolve the punishment cohort and loan-access risk set, reconcile policy clocks,
then retain separate loan and annual-outcome contracts. Existing knowledge is
unchanged; no paper PDF, credentials or restricted bank data were stored.

Add a bounded *Journal of Monetary Economics* finance/fiscal lane for the

of local government debt in China*, JME 159 (2026), article 103922,

[author research page](https://xuzhiwei09.wixsite.com/econ/research) supplies the

Candidate `candidate-e81fb679018b` preserves the loan-level contrast:

The province is the lender's location, not silently the borrower's address.
The paper uses one anonymous bank, 25 provinces and a 2013Q1-2017Q4 sample
excluding 2014Q4; Table 1's full baseline has 123,803 loans. Provinces' debt
stocks proxy exposure; actual branch-swapped debt is unavailable. Appendix
inspection, ownership coding, data lineage and extensive-margin denominators

The [MOF Q&A dated 12 March 2015](https://www.mof.gov.cn/zhengwuxinxi/caizhengxinwen/201503/t20150312_1201705.htm)
was inspected in full. Its first RMB 1 trillion quota applies to audited
government repayment obligations outstanding on 30 June 2013 and due in 2015,
with a uniform 53.8% allocation ratio across regions. It excludes debts repayable
from enterprises' own revenues. This narrower legal boundary must survive the
paper's broad institutional description. Resolve authorization, issuance and
annual estimation clocks, and primary risk-weight rules; do not assume every
LGFV loan was converted on 1 January 2015. Keep the 2015 swap separate from
2024 debt measures and the existing Basel III monetary-transmission variation.
Screen admits a candidate only; no canonical file or ready count is changed.

### JME debt-swap resolution — 2026-10-02

Task `task-53db0c4e99c8` admits
`china-2015-local-government-debt-swap-credit-reallocation` as grounded,
conditional knowledge. The State Council text was recovered through direct HTTP:
document date 21 September 2014, public release 2 October, Sections II/III/VI
separating government repayment debt from enterprises' own obligations. MOF's
December 2015 ministerial report corroborates the year's rollout and accompanying
issuance/liquidity measures. An official local-government capital-rule reprint
was inspected through Articles 47-68; Article 58 distinguishes qualifying public
claims from corporate investees. Anonymous-bank implementation remains attributed
to the article, not independently verified by that regulation.

Published article pages 12-17 and the data-availability/supplement passage on
page 18 were additionally inspected. The loan-rate contract uses lender province,
private/state status and end-2014 debt; 2015 is a reported annual post clock,
not an exact settlement date. Section 4.7 resolves the extensive-margin denominator

Performance*, JHR46(1),123-153,
[DOI10.3368/jhr.46.1.123](https://doi.org/10.3368/jhr.46.1.123).
The [Berkeley faculty publication page](https://gspp.berkeley.edu/research-and-impact/publications/the-contributions-of-school-quality-and-teacher-qualifications-to-student-p)
identifies the publication and links the
[September2009 forthcoming author manuscript](https://gspp.berkeley.edu/assets/uploads/research/pdf/SchoolEffectJHR.pdf).
Screen `task-c7225c0fe334` inspected this40page PDF in memory, pp1-21,25-29,
Sections3-4,5.1,6.1-6.2. It is not the inspected2011 typeset version. Publisher
page/DOI access failed. DOI/title/channel searches found no existing matching
canonical or task; the blocked2019 Dongcheng housing/zoning candidate concerns
a different cohort and assignment regime. This is one-paper coverage, not a
complete JHR archive audit or numerical journal ranking.

Reported mechanism: a1998 Eastern City District (Dongcheng), Beijing admission
reform, studied through the1999 entry cohort and2002 HSEE. Neighborhood-specific
quotas and ranked school choices determine successive oversubscription lotteries.
The paper constructs137 selection channels; random comparison is within channel,
not between arbitrary students or all Beijing schools. Its4717 first-choice
lottery participants are a subset of7102 enrolled students. Random numbers and
actual lottery outcomes are not in the administrative extract; school attendance,
choice records and transfer reports reconstruct compliance. The main school-effect
sample is2211 non-transfer students with scores/core covariates; the teacher
specification has1978 students/24schools. These are manuscript-specific counts.

earlier-step balance checks do not establish randomization for this residual group.
Observed and unidentified transfers, selective HSEE participation and failed
record linkage threaten the observed-score comparison. School-effect comparisons
are restricted to connected assignment markets (23schools and5schools), not a
common nationwide quality scale. School lottery assignment does not randomly
promote individual teachers: interpreting rank composition separately needs the
paper's additional restrictions. Its transfer robustness IV uses reconstructed
counterfactual school qualifications, not an observed lottery number.

Retain a china-variation candidate for separate resolve. Follow-up should verify
original1998/1999 district rules and school-neighborhood quota/choice contracts,
check the final publication against the author draft, recover data-access and join
limits, and retain lottery-channel, market, transfer and attrition restrictions.

the unrelated anonymous high-school ZX price-menu study. No canonical, restricted
microdata or paper file was stored.

### AER evaluator-identity experiment reopened — 2026-10-02

Screen `task-aea436e56d81` and resolve `task-e82dc65b8442` revisit
`10.1257/aer.20211207` under the broadened governance/labor scope. The August
Top5 map's shorthand "firm/worker experiment" misdescribes the population:
the published study concerns temporary public employees in the 三支一扶
programme. Preserve the dated narrow-scope exclusion, but do not reuse that
population label. This is a public-personnel incentive experiment, not an
agricultural production intervention, even though some participants have
agricultural-support posts.

The [final published article hosted by J-PAL](https://www.povertyactionlab.org/sites/default/files/research-paper/de-janvry-et-al-2023-subjective-performance-evaluation-influence-activities-and-bureaucratic-work-behavior-evidence.pdf)
was inspected in memory: AER113(3),766–799,2023; especially pp770–777 and
781–784. The reported experiment covers3,785 CGCSs in two provinces, cohorts
admitted in2016/2017. Scheme assignment is at the **work-unit** level, not
independently at employee level. Two-thirds enter the revealed scheme and
one-third the masked scheme. In both schemes one of two supervisors is
randomly selected as the final evaluator; only revealed-scheme employees learn
the identity at the outset. Supervisors and colleagues are not directly informed
of selection. September2017 baseline/notifications, January2018 reminder and
June2018 endline define the reported exposure, not a generic annual province
policy dummy.

The [published appendix](https://www.aeaweb.org/articles/materials/18225)
was inspected at PDF pp5–8,12 and29–30. TableA1 distinguishes baseline CGCS,
endline colleague/supervisor/CGCS surveys and government promotion records.
AppendixB's two sample notification letters describe an evaluator already drawn
and disclosed versus one to be drawn at the end. These are published translated
samples, not independently inspected original stamped government notices or
personnel allocation records. The article reports929 losses (24.5percent),
principally reassignment breaking supervisor relationships and exits; it reports
attrition diagnostics and Lee bounds. A nonsignificant attrition difference is
not evidence that missing outcomes are harmless. Table3's performance analysis
uses county, job-type and cohort effects with work-unit clustered errors;
colleague ratings are subjective performance proxies, not direct measures of
public-service output. Supervisor ratings and salary analyses have different
observed samples and should not be forced into one complete-case contract.

The primary [AEA registry AEARCTR-0002621](https://www.socialscienceregistry.org/trials/2621)
was inspected through a successful ordinary HTTP request after the web reader
returned403. Its General Information and Experimental Details specify computer
randomization at government-unit level, a planned4,000 employees/1,400 units,
five arms involving evaluator disclosure **and interim feedback**, and planned
September1,2017–August31,2018 intervention. Initial submission December7,2017
and publication December10 occurred after the reported September start; do not
describe this as verified registration before treatment. The registry uses CGVO
terminology whereas the final paper uses CGCS. These registered fields cannot
be substituted for realized sample, dates or allocation probabilities.

The [official replication deposit](https://doi.org/10.3886/E182787V1)
listing was inspected, including README.pdf and Code folder entries
`1_Data_Preparation.do`, `2_Tables.do` and `3_Appendix.do`. The README download
redirected to ICPSR login; no README content, code or data was inspected or
executed. The listing proves a deposit exists, not how planned arms map to the
final comparison. Full published-article text contains no reconciliation of the
registered feedback intervention. This discrepancy does not establish an error
in the paper, but it leaves open whether the recorded contrast is masking alone,
a selected pair of arms, or a pooled bundle of interventions.

Candidate `candidate-9c25408526ab` remains blocked rather than being published
as an extracted record or counted ready. Reopen with legitimately accessible
README/allocation code, implementation documentation or an author clarification
that maps the five planned arms to the published two schemes and distinguishes
feedback from masking. Then record one precise realized mechanism, lawful
employee/work-unit joins and outcome-specific missingness. Do not manufacture
province-year treatment, infer public participant identities, or split the
planned arms into several variations without evidence of their actual use.

### Development-oriented discovery boundary — 2026-10-02

The owner now prioritizes shocks directly relevant to mainland investment,
employment, productivity, incomes and industrial/regional development, rather
than narrow examination or public-personnel experiments. This changes upcoming
collection priorities; it does not remove existing knowledge or relax evidence.
The bounded recall pass covered the established Journal of Development Economics
lane, with publisher metadata and independent university/author recall for
electricity reliability and manufacturing performance. No journal-wide completion
claim follows from this subject search.

DOI `10.1016/j.jdeveco.2025.103541`, *High temperature, power rationing, and firm
performance*, JDE176(2025),103541, is a new screen lead after DOI/title comparison
against canonical, candidate and task ledgers. The publisher identifies Chinese
firm exposure and a precipitation-anomaly/interprovincial-hydropower supply
construction. A [CityU institutional final-PDF route](https://scholars.cityu.edu.hk/ws/portalfiles/portal/287960602/282848553.pdf)
was located, not yet inspected. Screen the actual final design before retaining
it: heat directly affects production, measured electricity use is not an assigned
outage, and hydropower weights plus remote rainfall require their own exclusion
and timing account. This source is promising for industrial-development decisions,
not yet a ready variation.

Screen `task-9881553e8f42` retrieved that24-page institutional PDF in memory.
The first page is a repository cover; the following23 pages are the published
article including appendices. The cover's article-number typo `130541` does
not override the publisher pages' `103541`. Sections4–6, article pp5–10,
Eqs10–13, Table4 and AppendixC/D pp20–22 were inspected. The retained object
is a hydropower-network-weighted precipitation supply exposure, not a2022
shutdown event or an observed firm rationing indicator.

Equation12 weights source-province precipitation anomalies with recipient
province hydropower dependence in year `t-1`. It uses cumulative rain in
months `m-3` through `m-2`, relative to the corresponding preceding ten-year
mean, where `m` is the recipient province's hottest month. Prose saying
"one month prior" is not a reason to replace this printed timing. Equation11
prints `e_jpt/(C_pt+Export_pt)` and asserts the weights sum to one; retain
the formula and reconcile its accounting/normalization against the actual
matrix rather than silently substituting a familiar import-share denominator.
Section4.3 reports a manually coded2006–2016 interprovincial transmission
matrix from *Compilation of Statistical Information on China's Electric Power
Industry*. Unknown transmission sources are allocated using the producing
province's hydro share. Primary matrix pages and reconstruction code have not
been inspected. Near-zero shock means or substantial variance do not establish
random assignment.

The reported manufacturing sample is171,270 firms/586,168 firm-years in the
2007–2016 National Tax Survey; Table4's hydropower application has585,093
observations. Section4 matches county-year weather from824 stations, gridded
at0.1degree, to standardized county IDs. AppendixC explains firm-name/address
and legal-ID linkage, changed industry codes and geocoding exclusions; these
are reported processing choices, not independently reproduced joins. Monthly
weather determines an annual exposure attached to annual outcomes, not observed
monthly interruptions. Electricity levels and TFP/output are different outcomes.

Table4/Eq13 uses firm and industry-year effects, with county clustering and
a province-clustered check. Baseline temperature-bin Eq9 instead includes
province-year effects; these would absorb the province-year hydropower shock
and must not be copied into its specification. Output/TFP Table6 uses heat
exposure, not a demonstrated two-stage causal effect of electricity rationing
on productivity. Section6.3 tests competing channels but cannot make all
rainfall, upstream supply-chain or heat effects disappear. A resolve task must
recover primary weather/transmission documentation, weights/scaling/calendar,
lawful data access and the conditional exclusion argument. Screen admission
does not raise canonical readiness.

### Hydropower-network resolve — 2026-10-02

Resolve `task-131edc3f3b7a` re-inspected the final publisher PDF in memory,
article pp5–8 and appendices. Direct access initially failed with a transient
TLS error; a subsequent direct request returned200. This is not evidence of
a permanently inaccessible article. The primary
[2011 NDRC rules](https://www.ndrc.gov.cn/xxgk/zcfb/tz/201104/W020190905514669845901.pdf)
were read, Articles3,5–12,20–26,32,35. They establish administrative/grid
responsibilities, selective protection/restriction, announcement obligations
and a2011-05-01 effective date. They do not establish the2007–2016 paper's
actual firm interruption schedules or hydropower weights; do not repackage
this continuous exposure as a2011 policy DID.

The decisive open construction gap is in published Eqs10–12, articlep7.
Eq10 defines consumption `C=G+Import-Export`; Eq11 divides incoming source
flows by `C+Export`, defines the diagonal as locally generated **and used**
hydropower and asserts weights sum to1. Under those written definitions,
incoming flows including retained local generation sum to `C`, implying
`sum(Dep)=C/(C+Export)` for a net exporting province, not1 [analytical
inference]. If the actual diagonal instead includes all generation, or the
code renormalizes, a different interpretation is possible. Neither was
verified. This is a reproducibility gap, not a demonstrated failure of the
paper's results. No substitute denominator was invented.

Eq12 identifies the recipient's hottest month **in the same year** using the
highest monthly mean temperature and source rainfall in `m-3:m-2`, weighted
by previous-year dependence. Its anomaly prose says a difference from the
corresponding ten-year average, while Table1 reports a standard deviation
of0.06 without an inspected scaling transformation. Source-province spatial
aggregation, year-boundary handling and code scaling remain unresolved.
Contemporaneous hottest-month selection also needs interpretation alongside
the claimed independence from demand. Eq13 has firm and industry-year effects,
not province-year effects, and is a supply-exposure regression rather than an
inspected two-stage outage effect on TFP.

The official CMA V3.0 catalog search lead identifies824 stations and registered
research access, but both mobile/desktop document routes failed on re-access;
it is not treated as inspected primary documentation here. Primary annual
dispatch tables and a public executable package were not recovered from the
publisher/author routes. Existing published appendix joins remain reported,
not reproduced. The candidate is blocked pending lawful matrix/code inspection
or a documented author clarification of weights, units and calendar. It is
not published as extracted or counted ready. Continue other development
sources without repeatedly reopening this blocker absent new evidence.

Screen `task-7367d5ede9c6` inspected the

evidence*, JPubE102(2013),13–22, DOI `10.1016/j.jpubeco.2013.03.005`.
Printed pp15–19, Sections2.2–4 and Eqs1–3 establish an actual firm-panel IV
application, not a generic product-rate DID. Prior DOI/title reconciliation
found no duplicate in canonical/tasks/candidates.

The endogenous variable is the province-by-two-digit-industry mean of de facto
firm rebate rates, reconstructed from accounting items and positive exports.
The instrument is `(administrative expenditure - business tax revenue) /
administrative expenditure` at province-year level. Main estimates use2004–2006;
2000–2002 is a pre-reform relevance comparison, with2003 omitted for arrears.
The sample selects firms exporting at least once during2000–2006. Province-industry
or firm clustering and firm/year effects are reported, not a randomly assigned
fiscal deficit. The accounting reconstruction assumes no bonded imported
inputs; excluding foreign/processing firms is a robustness check, not proof
that all exporters' rebates are observed directly.

Resolve must inspect original2003/2005 cost-sharing notices, distinguish the
fixed2003 base from the paper's later loose previous-year wording, and reconcile
the2005 reduction in the local financing share with its2004–2006 application.
Local service demand and fiscal spending can directly affect exports, so the
IV exclusion remains conditional even with a strong first stage. The fiscal
burden mechanism is distinct from statutory product rebate-rate changes.

government gazette, Jiangsu's2004 implementation and MOF/SAT/PBOC's2005
budget notice. The fixed2003 base and original25percent local liability are

exposure and explicitly places the2005 procedure outside that regime. The
paper's2004–2006 application remains conditional on regime-specific relevance,
fiscal exclusion, accounting reconstruction and lawful firm-data access. No

known records in the top20 of a growing collection. The bounded repair makes

equivalent English query; fixed-input ranking tests remain unchanged. Search

does preclude treating an opened city's exposure as requiring its own local

https://business.sohu.com/20041201/n223266774.shtml is a further lead for

test of the central POE-minus-SOE interaction. Nonsignificant separate subgroup
coefficients neither prove a zero difference nor verify the main differential
estimate's heterogeneity robustness. Appendix A/C contains cleaning and further
comparisons, but the exact publisher supplement route returned403; those
appendix results remain reported, not inspected. No executable code or full
actual-opening city roster was recovered.

The candidate remains blocked pending a reconciled actual-versus-commitment

### Property Law resolution: preserved draft, missing final design — 2026-10-02

Resolve `task-3607aa0b79da` follows candidate `candidate-a88a045522ed`.
The NBER manuscript was additionally inspected in memory at printed pp15–18
and pp40,42–48, including Tables1–8. Table1 defines the national Law dummy

explanatory characteristics in2006; this closes the draft's previously open
measurement-date question, not the published article's question. The event
sample has777 observations, with753 in specifications requiring cash flow.
Investment is ending minus beginning net fixed assets plus depreciation,
divided by lagged net fixed assets; annual regressions use lagged Q or lagged
stock returns and firm-clustered inference. CSMAR firm/security identifiers,
financial year and trading date are the necessary linkage dimensions, but an
executable extraction and identifier mapping were not obtained.

Table5 corrects a possible misreading of the text's fixed-effect description:
columns1 and5 estimate the standalone Law coefficient **without** year
dummies; other columns use year dummies and differential interactions, not
that standalone coefficient. Table7 likewise omits year dummies in its first
column. There is no demonstrated collinearity defect in those tables. The
standalone before/after coefficient still lacks a national untreated
counterfactual; interactions require credible differential-response assumptions
and do not turn firm characteristics into randomly assigned treatment.

The original law was inspected on the
[Beijing government reproduction](https://www.beijing.gov.cn/zhengce/zhengcefagui/qtwj/200710/t20071026_780682.html),
header and Articles4,42,170–174,180–189,193,247. Passage is March16,2007;
effectiveness is October1. Article173 specifies secured claim coverage subject
to contractual variation. Registration creates the specified immovable
mortgages under187, whereas188–189 distinguish movable mortgage creation
from opposability to good-faith third parties. Do not collapse these rules
into universal registration-based treatment. These provisions establish
formal rights, not observed court enforcement or credit availability.

explicitly has no associated files. The publisher main and abstract routes
failed to open as inspectable text. Indexed publisher previews point to
December29,2006, rather than the draft's March16,2007 event. This is a

https://www.npc.gov.cn/c2/c189/c222/201905/t20190522_92493.html failed by

history https://www.gov.cn/2007lh/content_545237.htm also failed to open.
Their indexed summaries suggest referral of the draft to the plenary on
December29, not legal effectiveness; actual passage inspection is still needed.

The bounded resolution ends blocked at canonical admission, without discarding

### Firm innovation / legal-regime resolution — 2026-10-02

Resolve `task-da2f58850c99` verified the Enterprise Bankruptcy Law through
the [CSRC-hosted full text](https://www.csrc.gov.cn/csrc_en/c102033/c1371259/content.shtml),
header and Articles1–2: the Standing Committee adopted it August27,2006 and

bankruptcy and creditor/debtor claims. Together with the Property Law's
March16 passage and October1 effectiveness, the statutes cannot be assigned
the same legal date simply because both affect collateral or the paper indexes
the sample to calendar years. For firm-year outcomes, 2007 is a mixed exposure
year: the bankruptcy regime is effective for more of the year than the Property
Law. Which event the paper intends its Post indicator to represent is a design
question, not settled by its abstract.

Author and institutional publication pages confirm the Research Policy
publication and coauthors. Publisher-indexed full-text passages state a

publication pages expose no manuscript attachment. Accordingly we do not
claim to have inspected the timing of pre-reform tangibility, exact Post
definition, instrument construction, patent-firm matching, or regression
tables. The 2019 university seminar abstract is contemporaneous but remains
an abstract, not the full design.

The two candidates represent distinct *paper applications* but possibly the
same national Property Law episode. Do not count them as two variations.
